ing Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'IsFix'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnIsFix = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = '' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS SMALLINT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'WHId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnWHId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0) = 0
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'IsAutoBarcode'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnIsAutoBarcode = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0)  = 0 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS BIT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'tblTmpBarcodeId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0)  = 0 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'SerialId'/*NKR14020921*/
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnSerialId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0)  = 0 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'ExpDate'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnExpDate = CASE WHEN @ItmSnFieldValue IS NULL
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS DATETIME) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'ExpDatePersian'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnExpDatePersian = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(8)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoI'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoII'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoII = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoIII'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoIII = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoIV'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoIV = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoV'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoV = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoVI'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoVI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											SET @DocItemSn = SUBSTRING(@DocItemSn,
														LEN(@ItmSnField + '|') + 1,
														LEN(@DocItemSn))
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/											
										/**/
									END
									ELSE
									BEGIN
										SET @ItmSnField = @DocItemSn
										SET @DocItemSn = NULL						
									END
								END									
								/*=========================================================*/
								BEGIN TRAN	
									/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
									DECLARE @OprType INT,@DocISerialIdFound INT
									SET @OprType = (SELECT OprType FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE DocOprID = @OprId)
									/*Check the serial Id */											
									/*RZ990519- End*/
									/*select @ItmSnID,@ItmId*/
									/*IF ((@ItmSnID = @ItmId) AND ((SELECT DIS.DocItemSerialID FROM WSDocItemSerials DIS WHERE DIS.ItemId = @InsertedDocItemId AND DIS.Serial = @ItmSnSerial) IS NULL) AND (ISNULL(@ItmSnSerial, '') <> ''))*/
									IF ((@ItmSnID = @ItmId))
									BEGIN
										/**/
												
										DECLARE @InsertedItemSerialId INT

										INSERT INTO WSDocItemSerials(
													[ItemId],
													[Serial],
													[StatusId],
													[Description],
													[IsFix],
													[WHId],
													[SerialId],
													[IsAutoBarcode],
													[tblTmpBarcodeId],
													[ExpDate],
													[ExpDatePersian],
													[InfoI],
													[InfoII],
													[InfoIII],
													[InfoIV],
													[InfoV],
													[InfoVI],
													[DocISLinkAccItemSerialId],
													[FPId],
													[FirstRegisterUserId],
													[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
													[FirstRegisterDate],
													[UserId],
													[Month1],
													[RegisterDate],
													[RegisterDatePersian],
													[AppType],
													[LanguageID])
											VALUES (
													@InsertedDocItemId,
													@ItmSnSerial,
													@ItmSnStatusId,
													@ItmSnDescription,
													@ItmSnIsFix,
													@ItmSnWHId,
													@ItmSnSerialId,
													@ItmSnIsAutoBarcode,
													@ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId,
													@ItmSnExpDate,
													@ItmSnExpDatePersian,
													@ItmSnInfoI,
													@ItmSnInfoII,
													@ItmSnInfoIII,
													@ItmSnInfoIV,
													@ItmSnInfoV,
													@ItmSnInfoVI,
													NULL,
													@FPId,
													@UserId,
													@RegisterDatePersian,
													@RegisterDate,
													@UserId,
													@Month1,
													@RegisterDate,
													@RegisterDatePersian,
													@AppType,
													@DefaultLanguageID
											)

										SET @InsertedItemSerialId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
										
										/*=================== Start Multi Language [en] ===================*/
										/*======================= Start Add Columns =======================*/			
						
										INSERT INTO WSDocItemSerials_Lang(
											[DocItemSerialID],
											[LanguageID],
											[Description],
											[InfoI],
											[InfoII],
											[InfoIII],
											[InfoIV],
											[InfoV],
											[InfoVI]
											)
										SELECT 
											@InsertedItemSerialId,
											LN.LanguageID,
											@Description,
											@ItmSnInfoI,
											@ItmSnInfoII,
											@ItmSnInfoIII,
											@ItmSnInfoIV,
											@ItmSnInfoV,
											@ItmSnInfoVI
										FROM Languages LN
										WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID 
										AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive, 0) = 1
																
										/*======================= End Add Columns =======================*/
										/*=================== End Multi Language [en] ===================*/
			
									END
									/**/
								IF (@@ERROR > 0)
									BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert DocItemSerials' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
								ELSE 
									COMMIT TRAN 
																										
							/**/
							END
						/**/
						FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItemSn INTO  @DocItemSn
						END
						CLOSE CrsrItemSn
						DEALLOCATE CrsrItemSn
						
						/**/
					END

					/*=================== End Insert Doc Item Serial ===================*/
				END
				/**/	
					
				/*============================ End Insert Item ============================*/
							
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO  @DocItem
		END
		CLOSE CrsrItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrItem
		/**/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert DocItems' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 	

		/*======================= End Insert Doc Item ========================*/
		/**/
	END /* END = IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '') */
	/*==============================*/
	IF((ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'' ) <> ''))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 1
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF((ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'' ) = ''))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	/**/
	END
	
	/*===========================*/
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0 OR (ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'' ) <> ''))
	BEGIN 
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 0
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	END	
	/**/
END







GO

Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispIssueDraftFromOrder]
(
	/*

			@LanguageID = 1065,
			@OrdItemList = N'3113,5|3114,3|3115,2|3116,0|3117,4|3118,4|3120,0'

		select @InsertedDraftID,@InsertedDraftNumber
	*/
	@OrderID INT,
	@DraftDate VARCHAR(8),
	@UserId INT,
	@Month1 INT,
	@WHId INT,
	@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
	@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
	@InsertedDraftID INT OUTPUT ,
	@InsertedDraftNumber INT OUTPUT,
	/*ADBF951130*/
	@LanguageID INT ,
	@OrdItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) /*= NULL*//*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/ /*RZ14000908- Remove null in parameters*/
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @FPId INT, @Year1 SMALLINT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
SET @Year1 = @FPId
/**/
/*---------------------- Set TmpTabel -------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
	[OrderItemId] INT,
	[Quantity] FLOAT
)
IF(ISNULL(@OrdItemList,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @CrsrOrderItemId INT,@CrsrQuantity FLOAT,@Text NVARCHAR(MAX)
	/**/
	DECLARE CrsrI CURSOR LOCAL FOR
	/**/	
		SELECT SplitData  
		FROM fnSplitString(@OrdItemList, '|')
	/**/
	OPEN CrsrI
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*================================================================================*/
		DECLARE @Field NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
		SET @CrsrOrderItemId = NULL
		SET @CrsrQuantity = NULL			
		/**/
		/*****************************************************************/
		IF(ISNULL(@Text,'') <> '') 
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*===========================================================*/	
			SET @CrsrOrderItemId = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, 1, CHARINDEX(',',@Text)-1),'0') AS INT)                                    
			SET @CrsrQuantity = CAST(ISNULL(SUBSTRING(@Text, CHARINDEX(',', @Text)+1, Len(@Text)),'0') AS FLOAT)

			INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([OrderItemId],[Quantity])
			VALUES(@CrsrOrderItemId,@CrsrQuantity)
								
			/*===========================================================*/				
		/**/
		END
		/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
	/**/	
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrI INTO @Text
	END
	CLOSE CrsrI
	DEALLOCATE CrsrI
/**/
END
/*RZ14000628- End*/

/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertedOldDraftID INT
/*SELECT @InsertedOldDraftID = DocID FROM dbo.WSDoc Doc WHERE Doc.OrderId = @OrderID
IF @InsertedOldDraftID > 0 RETURN -1*/ 
/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
/*���� ���� ���� � �ǘ��� ����� �� ����� ���� �ѐ� ����� ���� ����  �� ������ ������ ����� �����  ����� �� ���.
 ���� �� ���� �ѐ� ����� �� ����� ������ ���� �� �ǘ��� ���� ���.*/
IF(ISNULL((
	ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ordIQuantity,0)) 
			FROM dbo.WSOrderItems 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
			/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
			AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
			/*RZ14020606- End*/
			),0)
	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(Quantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSDocItems DI WHERE DI.DocId IN(SELECT D.DocId FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderID)),0)
	),0) = 0
)
BEGIN
	RETURN -1
END
/*RZ14000628*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
DECLARE @DocDateTime DATETIME, @TimeNow TIME
SET @DocDateTime = (SELECT dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@DraftDate, null))
SET @TimeNow = CONVERT(TIME, GETDATE())
SET @DocDateTime = @DocDateTime + CAST(@TimeNow AS DATETIME)
/*RZ980703- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @DraftNumber INT
/*Select @DraftNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(
(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = 
(SELECT OrderOprId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId) ) )
SET @InsertedDraftNumber = @DraftNumber*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ980710- Move OrdDescII to Doc*/
DECLARE @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc INT
IF EXISTS (
SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromOrder' )
AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actMoveOrdDescriptionIIToDoc'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmIssueDraftFromOrder' ))
AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 1
ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 0
	
/*RZ980710- END*/

/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*--********************MAINTRAN*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
/*RZ960225 dalile jabejaie declare @DraftId dar in hast ke dar multi language ham be draftid jadid niaz darim*/
Declare @DraftId INT 
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION START ------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @WareHouseId INT
	DECLARE Crsr_Wrhs CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
		SELECT DISTINCT 
			ordiWHId
		FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderID 
		/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
		AND OrderItemID IN(SELECT TT.OrderItemId FROM @TmpTable TT WHERE ISNULL(TT.Quantity,0) > 0)
		/*RZ14000628- End*/		
		/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
		AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
							FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
							WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
		/*RZ14020606- End*/
	OPEN Crsr_Wrhs
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN		
		SET @DraftId = NULL
		SET @InsertedDraftID = NULL
		SET @DraftNumber = NULL
		SET @InsertedDraftNumber = NULL
		/*---------------- Doc Number ----------------*/
		SELECT @DraftNumber =  dbo.WSfnReturnDocNumber(
								(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = 
								(SELECT OrderOprId FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId) ) )
		SET @InsertedDraftNumber = @DraftNumber
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------- tran1 ----------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*------------------- tran1*/
		/*RZ980710- Move OrdDescII to Doc*/	
		IF @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 1
		BEGIN
		/*---------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDoc (
				OprId,
				DocNumber,
				DocDate,
				DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				/*--BuyOrderId,*/
				SendAddress, 
				IsFix,	
				WHId,
				UserId,	
				Month1,	
				FPId, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				FirstRegisterUserId, 
				FirstRegisterDate, 
				CompanyId,
				CompanyPersonId,
				ProjectId, 
				AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				LanguageID,
				FirstRegisterDatePersian 
				,[Description]
			) 	
			SELECT  
				(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID), 
				@DraftNumber,
				@DraftDate,
				/*GETDATE(),*/
				@DocDateTime,/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
				OrderId,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId), 
				1,
				/*@WhID,*/
				ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
				@UserId, 
				@Month1, 
				@FPId, 
				GETDATE(), 
				@RegisterDatePersian, 
				@UserId,
				GETDATE(), 
				ordCompanyId,
				ordCompanyPersonId,
				ordProjectId, 
				@AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				@LanguageID, 
				@RegisterDatePersian
				,[ordDescriptionII]		 
			FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
			/*RZ960225 for multi language And add length variable*/
			/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
			/*RZ960225 dar inja nesbat dehi be @DraftId anjam mishavad*/
			SELECT @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
				(
				   [DocID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[SendAddress]
				   ,[Description]
				 )
			SELECT 
				@DraftId,
				OL.LanguageID,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId)  AND CYLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID)
				,OL.[ordDescriptionII]
			FROM dbo.WSOrder_Lang OL 
			INNER JOIN dbo.WSOrder O ON OL.OrderId = O.OrderId
			WHERE O.OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/ 
		/*---------------------------------*/
		END/*RZ980710- END*/
		ELSE IF @HavePermissionMoveOrdDescIIToDoc = 0
		BEGIN
		/*---------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSDoc (
				OprId,
				DocNumber,
				DocDate,
				DocDateTime,
				OrderId,
				/*--BuyOrderId,*/
				SendAddress, 
				IsFix,	
				WHId,
				UserId,	
				Month1,	
				FPId, 
				RegisterDate, 
				RegisterDatePersian, 
				FirstRegisterUserId, 
				FirstRegisterDate, 
				CompanyId,
				CompanyPersonId,
				ProjectId, 
				AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				LanguageID,
				FirstRegisterDatePersian 
			) 	
			SELECT  
				(SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr op WHERE op.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID), 
				@DraftNumber,
				@DraftDate,
				/*GETDATE(),*/
				@DocDateTime,/*RZ980703- Edit DocDateTime*/
				OrderId,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId)) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = ordCompanyId), 
				1,
				/*@WhID,*/
				ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
				@UserId, 
				@Month1, 
				@FPId, 
				GETDATE(), 
				@RegisterDatePersian, 
				@UserId,
				GETDATE(), 
				ordCompanyId,
				ordCompanyPersonId,
				ordProjectId, 
				@AppType,
				/*ADBF951130*/
				@LanguageID, 
				@RegisterDatePersian		 
			FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
			/*RZ960225 for multi language And add length variable*/
			/*-------------- Begin insert to lang table ---------*/
			/*RZ960225 dar inja nesbat dehi be @DraftId anjam mishavad*/
			SELECT @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
				(
				   [DocID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[SendAddress]
				 )
			SELECT 
				@DraftId,
				OL.LanguageID,
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = 
					(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId)  AND CYLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceId = 
					(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId) AND PLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID) 
				+ ' '+
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId AND CCLN.LanguageID = OL.LanguageID)
			FROM dbo.WSOrder_Lang OL 
			INNER JOIN dbo.WSOrder O ON OL.OrderId = O.OrderId
			WHERE O.OrderId = @OrderId
			/*-------------- End insert to lang table -----------*/ 
		/*---------------------------------*/
		END
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSDoc' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------
		--===================================================================
		--===================================================================
		--===================================================================*/
		/*Declare @DraftId INT 
		Select @DraftId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()*/
		/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
		/*RZ960225 dalile jabejaie declare @DraftId dar in hast ke dar multi language ham be DraftId jadid niaz darim*/
		/*chon dar zaman darj draft be @DraftId id jadid nesbat dade shode inja be 2 dastor bala niazi nist*/
		SET @InsertedDraftID = @DraftId
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*---------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL, @ordIPrdId INT = NULL, @ordIQuantity float = NULL /*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
			,@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @ordIWHId INT = NULL
			,@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL/*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
			,@ordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			,@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
			,@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL/*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			 /*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			 ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL
			 ,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT  = NULL
			, @TmpI FLOAT = NULL
			 /*RZ14010621- End*/
			 ,@ordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*--*/
			DECLARE crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR /*--For OrderItems*/
				SELECT 	
					OrderItemID,
					ordIPrdId,
					ordIQuantity,
					ordIDescription,
					ordIFCR, ordIFCRI, /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
					ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
					,ordIPrdUnitId/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
					,ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
					/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
					,ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					/*RZ14010621- End*/
					,ordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				FROM dbo.WSOrderItems
				WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
				AND ordIWHId = @WareHouseId /*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
				/*RZ14020606- Not Add Product by prdKind 2*/
				AND ordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
								  WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )
				/*RZ14020606- End*/
			/*--*/
			OPEN crsr
			FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, @ordIDescription,
									  @ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI, /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
									  @ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
									  ,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
									  ,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
									  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
									  ,@ordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*--*/
			WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 
			BEGIN
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------------- WSDocItems --------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
				IF(EXISTS(SELECT * FROM @TmpTable))
				BEGIN
				/**/
					DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
					SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP 1 [Quantity] FROM @TmpTable WHERE OrderItemId = @OrderItemID)
					IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/	
						FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, @ordIDescription,
												  @ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI, @ordITracing,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
												  ,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
												  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
												  ,@ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
						CONTINUE
					/**/
					END
					ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
					BEGIN
					/**/						
						/*RZ14011005- Calculate QuantityTotal*/
						IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,0) <> ISNULL(@CurrQty,0))
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @ordIQuantityTotal = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
						/**/
						END
						/*RZ14011005- End*/
						SET @ordIQuantity = @CurrQty
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14000628- End*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
				SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
				SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
				SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
				SET @TmpI = NULL
				/*--------------*/			
				SELECT 				
					@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
					@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
					@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE ProductId = @ordIPrdId
				/*--------------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 					
				/**/
				END
				/*----------------------------------------------------*/
				IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,1)*/
					SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)/*RZ14010629- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/

					IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14010621- End*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems (
					DocId,
					PrdId,
					Quantity,
					[Description],
					UserId,	Month1,	FPId, RegisterDate, RegisterDatePersian,
					WHId, AppType,
					/*ADBF951130*/
					LanguageID,
					FirstRegisterDatePersian,
					FirstRegisterUserId,
					FirstRegisterDate
					,[FCR], [FCRI] /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */ 
					,[Tracing]/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
					,[PrdUnitId] /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
					,[QuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
					/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
					,[PrdUnitIdI]
					,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
					/*RZ14010621- End*/	
					,[PrdNickname]  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					,[OrderItemId] /*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
				) 
				VALUES(
					@DraftId,
					@ordIPrdId,
					@ordIQuantity,
					@ordIDescription,
					@UserId, @Month1, @FPId, GETDATE(), @RegisterDatePersian,
					/*@WhID,*/
					ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
					@AppType,
					/*ADBF951130*/
					@LanguageID,
					@RegisterDatePersian,
					@UserId,
					GETDATE()
					,@ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */ 
					,@ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
					,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
					,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
					/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					/*RZ14010621- End*/	
					,@ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					,@OrderItemID /*RZ14021202- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
					)
				/*--*/
				DECLARE @DocItemId INT = NULL
				SELECT @DocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
				/*Begin insert records in lang table*/
				INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItems_Lang (
					[DocItemID]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[Description]	
				   ,[PrdNickname]  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				) 
				SELECT
					@DocItemId,
					OILN.LanguageID,
					OILN.ordIDescription,
					OILN.ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				FROM dbo.WSOrderItems_Lang OILN 
				WHERE  OILN.OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
				/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
				/*End insert records in lang table*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------------- WSDocItemSerials --------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
				BEGIN TRAN
				/*------------------- tran3 ---------------------*/
					/*RZ960225 for multi language */
					/*----------------- Begin user cursor for insert serial in doc -------------------*/
					/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @SerialId INT = NULL
					/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
					DECLARE @ordItemSerialDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					DECLARE @OrderItemSerialID INT = NULL
					DECLARE @docItemSerialId INT = NULL
					/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
					DECLARE Crsr_Add_Serial CURSOR LOCAL For
						/*RZ14000628- Insert docItemSerials to number  (@ordIQuantity)*/
						SELECT  TOP (CAST(@ordIQuantity AS INT))/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
							SerialId,
							[Description],
							OrderItemSerialID	
						FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials OSI
						WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
						/*RZ14000628- Add manual quantity for every product in multi draft*/
						AND SerialId NOT IN(SELECT   DIS.SerialId
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
											WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
																WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId
																AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																				FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																				WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderId
																				AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN(2,3)))
										
						
															 )											
											)
						AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT DISI.Serial FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DISI WHERE DISI.DocItemSerialID = OSI.SerialId),'') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
											NOT IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DIS.Serial AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
													FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
													WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																		FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
																		WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId
																		AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																						WHERE D.OrderId = @OrderId
																						AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1))																
																		)																
								)
						
						/*RZ14000628- End*/
					OPEN Crsr_Add_Serial
					FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO @SerialId, @ordItemSerialDescription, @OrderItemSerialID
					WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
					BEGIN
					/*---------------------------------------*/
						/*RZ14000325- Add rootId and Serial*/
						DECLARE @Serial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@RootId INT = NULL,@OprType INT = NULL
						/*Find doc type via orderOpr*/
						SET @OprType = (SELECT TOP 1[OprType]
											FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr]  DO
											WHERE DO.[DocOprID]  = ((SELECT  
																		(SELECT TOP 1 Opr.DocOprId 
																			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr WHERE Opr.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID) 			 
																		FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																		WHERE OrderId = @OrderId))						
										)
						/*check doc type*/
						IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 1)/*Doc*/
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SELECT TOP 1 
								 @Serial = [Serial]	
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
							WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @SerialId
							SET @RootId = 0
							SET @SerialId = NULL
						/**/
						END
						ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 2 OR  ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 3)/*Draft Or Movement*/
						BEGIN
						/**/
							/*----------------*/
							SELECT TOP 1 
								 @Serial = [Serial]							
								,@RootId = [RootId]
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
							WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @SerialId
							SET @RootId = ISNULL(@RootId,0)
							IF(@RootId = 0)
							BEGIN
								SET @RootId = @SerialId
							END
							/*ELSE IF(@RootId <> 0)
							BEGIN
								SET @RootId = @RootId
							END*/
							/*----------------*/
						/**/
						END						 			
						
						/*RZ14000325- End*/
						
						INSERT INTO dbo.WSDocItemSerials (
							ItemId,
							SerialId,
							[Description],
							WHId,
							UserId,	
							Month1,	
							FPId, 
							RegisterDate, 
							RegisterDatePersian, 
							AppType,
							/*ADBF951130*/
							LanguageID,
							FirstRegisterDatePersian,
							FirstRegisterUserId,
							FirstRegisterDate,
							/*RZ14000325- Add rootId and Serial*/
							[Serial],
							[RootId]
							/*RZ14000325*/)	
						VALUES( 
							@DocItemId,
							@SerialId,
							@ordItemSerialDescription,
							/*@WhID,*/
							ISNULL(@WareHouseId,@WhID),/*RZ14000226- Add the possibility of issuing multiple docs for different warehouses*/
							@UserId, 
							@Month1, 
							@FPId, 
							GETDATE(), 
							@RegisterDatePersian, 
							@AppType,
							/*ADBF951130*/
							@LanguageID,
							@RegisterDatePersian,
							@UserId,
							GETDATE(),
							/*RZ14000325- Add rootId and Serial*/
							@Serial,
							@RootId
							/*RZ14000325*/)
		
						SET @docItemSerialId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*Insert records in lang table*/			
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
						(
							[DocItemSerialID]
						   ,[LanguageID]
						   ,[Description]
						)
						SELECT
							@docItemSerialId,
							OISLN.LanguageID,
							OISLN.[Description]
						FROM dbo.WSOrderItemSerials_Lang OISLN 
						WHERE  OISLN.OrderItemSerialID = @OrderItemSerialID
		
						FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Serial INTO  @SerialId, @ordItemSerialDescription, @OrderItemSerialID
					/*---------------------------------------*/
					END
					CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
					DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
					/*----------------- End user cursor for insert serial in doc --------------------------------*/				
					/*------------------- tran3 -----------------------*/
					IF (@@ERROR > 0)
					BEGIN 
						SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItemSerial' 
						SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
						/*RZ960225 for multi language*/
						CLOSE Crsr_Add_Serial
						DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Serial
					ROLLBACK TRAN 
					END		
					ELSE 
					COMMIT TRAN 
					/*------------------- tran3 -----------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM crsr INTO @OrderItemID, @ordIPrdId, @ordIQuantity, 
										  @ordIDescription,@ordIFCR, @ordIFCRI, /*RZ980729- Add FCR,FCRI to docItem */
										  @ordITracing/*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
										  ,@ordIPrdUnitId /*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
										  ,@ordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
										  ,@ordIPrdUnitIdI,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010621- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
										  ,@ordIPrdNickname  /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			END
			/*--*/
			CLOSE crsr
			DEALLOCATE crsr
			/*--*/
			/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN 
				SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into DocItems' 
				SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
			ROLLBACK TRAN 
			END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
			/*------------------- tran2 ----------------------*/			
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Wrhs INTO @WareHouseId
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Wrhs
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Wrhs
		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14000226- End*/

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	Set @DraftId = 0
	Return @DraftId
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	Return @DraftId
END	
/*--*/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[ApispIssueOrder]





/*RZ980417*/
/*RZ980812- Add person when pass null*/
/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
/*RZ990818- Add ordITracing*/
/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
/*RZ14010919- Add PrdLinked and PrjPrdHis and ordCurrencyUnitId*/
/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
/*NKR14020729- Add Parameters*/
/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
/*RZ14021005- Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispIssueOrder]
/*
	exec ApispIssueOrder		 								
*/
(
/*Info for Login*/
@UserName NVARCHAR(50),
@Password NVARCHAR(50),
/*Order*/
@OrderOprId INT = NULL,
@PreOrderId INT = NULL,
@PreOrderVerifierId INT = NULL,
@OrdTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdNumber BIGINT = NULL,
@OrdDate NVARCHAR(8),
@OrdDateTime DATETIME,
@OrdDay NVARCHAR(50),
@OrdTime NVARCHAR(50),
@OrdSubject NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdInstallAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdTaxIndex NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@OrdIsVerified BIT = NULL,
@OrdVerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdVerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@OrdIsFixed  BIT = NULL,
@OrdGuarantyId INT = NULL,
@OrdSpecialWord NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdDescriptionI NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@OrdDescriptionII NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@OrdDescriptionId INT = NULL,
@OrdPrintFormatId INT = NULL,
@OrdRLogoId  INT = NULL,
@OrdLLogoId  INT = NULL,
@OrdSigningI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdSigningII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdSigningIII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountShabaNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdAccountShabaNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdSignatureId INT = NULL,
@OrdVerifierId INT = NULL,
@OrdSettlementTypeId INT = NULL,
@OrdTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdReferUserId INT = NULL,
@OrdDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdDiscountTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OrdNote NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@OrdcmpTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@OrdSerial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@OrdVerifierRequestId INT = NULL,
@OrdStatusId INT = 1,
/*Company AND Person AND Project*/
@CompanyId INT = NULL,
@CompanyNumber INT = NULL,
@CompanyCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
@ProjectId INT = NULL,
@ProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
/*Product*/
@ProductJson NVARCHAR(MAX),
/*Payment*/
@PaymentJson NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
/*Warehouse*/
@WHId INT = NULL,
/**/
@DefualtProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100),
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(50),
@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(50),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedOrderId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedOrderNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
/*RZ14010919- Add PrdLinked and PrjPrdHis and ordCurrencyUnitId*/
@AddLinkedPrd BIT = NULL,
@AppPrjPrdHis BIT = NULL,
@ordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
/*RZ14010919- End*/

/*NKR14020729- Start*/
@OrdDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@OrdDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
/*NKR14020729- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/************************************ WHId **************************************/
IF(@WHId IS NULL OR @WHId <= 0)
	SET @WHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 0)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT

/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
DECLARE @ordTspSettingId INT 
SET @ordTspSettingId = (SELECT TOP(1) TspSettingId FROM [dbo].[TspSetting])
/*RZ14020419- End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*WHID*/
IF(@WHId IS NOT NULL AND @WHId > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @wId INT
	SET @wId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE [WareHouseID] = @WHId)
	IF(@wId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WhId|'
END
/*OPRID*/
IF(@OrderOprId IS NOT NULL AND @OrderOprId > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @OprId INT
	SET @OprId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderOprID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] WHERE [OrderOprID] = @OrderOprId)
	IF(@OprId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OprId|'
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @OrderOprId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderOprID] FROM dbo.WSOrderOpr OO ORDER BY OrderView, OrderOprId )
END
/*PREODERID*/
IF(@PreOrderId IS NOT NULL AND @PreOrderId > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PordId INT
	SET @PordId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderId] = @PreOrderId)
	IF(@PordId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordId|'
END
/*PREORDERVERIFIERID*/
IF(@PreOrderVerifierId IS NOT NULL AND @PreOrderVerifierId > 0)
BEGIN

IF(@ordCurrencyUnitId IS NOT NULL AND @ordCurrencyUnitId > 0)

	SET @OrdCUId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @ordCurrencyUnitId)

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No ordCurrencyUnitId|'
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @ordCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
								  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
								  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
										= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								  )
END

			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupOrdNumber= ' + CAST(ISNULL(@DupOrdNumber,0) AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'

				SET @ProjectId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
						  WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
						  AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like '%' + 
						  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ProjectTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						  NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'))

				IF(@ProjectId IS NULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId From dbo.CrmProjects WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY [prjOrderView] DESC,prjStartDate DESC,[ProjectID] DESC)/*RZ14021005- Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/

					END

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN
		/*=============================== Order ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*---------------------------------------------------*/

			/*RZ14011109-End*/

			/*Main table*/

				   ([OrderOprID]

		/*=============================== Order Items ====================*/			
		/**/
		DECLARE @OrdIPrdId INT,
		@OrdIPrdCode NVARCHAR(50),
		@OrdIQuantity FLOAT,
		@OrdIPrice NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdITotalPrice  NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdIDescription NVARCHAR(4000),

		@OrdIDiscountI  NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdIDiscount  NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdIDiscountIPercent  NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdIPrdUnitId INT,
		@OrdICurrencyUnitId INT,
		@OrdIOrderView INT,
		@OrdIFCR FLOAT,
		@OrdITax   NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdITaxIndex  NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdICalculateTax BIT,
		@OrdINotVisible BIT,
