		@OrdIPricePartII NUMERIC(18,2),
		@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID INT,/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
		@OrdItem NVARCHAR(MAX),
		/*RZ990818- Add ordITracing*/
		@OrdITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
		/*RZ990818- End*/
		/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
		@OrdIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,
		@OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
		@OrdIValidityExpireDay INT = NULL,
		@OrdIValidityExpireHour INT = NULL,
		@OrdIValidityExpireMinute INT = NULL,
		/*RZ14010926- End*/
		/*NKR14020729- Start*/
		@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL,
		@OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryEndDate DATETIME = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)= NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
		@OrdIDeliveryUserId INT = NULL
		/*NKR14020729- End*/
		/**/
		BEGIN TRAN
		DECLARE CrsrItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR 		
			SELECT 
			(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
								FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@ProductJson) PJIN
								WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
								FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
			FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@ProductJson) PJ
			WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
		OPEN CrsrItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO @OrdItem
		/**/
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN		
		/**/
			DECLARE @InsertedOrderItemId INT
			/**/
			SET @OrdIPrdId = NULL
			SET @OrdIPrdCode  = NULL
			SET @OrdIQuantity  = NULL
			SET @OrdIPrice  = NULL
			SET @OrdITotalPrice   = NULL
			SET @OrdIDescription = NULL
			SET @OrdIQuantityDesc  = NULL
			SET @OrdIDiscountI   = NULL
			SET @OrdIDiscount  = NULL
			SET @OrdIDiscountPercent   = NULL
			SET @OrdIDiscountIPercent   = NULL
			SET @OrdIPrdUnitId  = NULL
			SET @OrdICurrencyUnitId   = NULL
			SET @OrdIOrderView   = NULL
			SET @OrdIFCR   = NULL
			SET @OrdITax    = NULL
			SET @OrdITaxIndex   = NULL
			SET @OrdICalculateTax   = NULL
			SET @OrdINotVisible   = NULL
			SET @OrdIFCRI   = NULL
			SET @OrdINotInSum   = NULL
			SET @OrdIRound    = NULL
			SET @OrdIRoundPlus    = NULL
			SET @OrdIPricePartRate   = NULL
			SET @OrdIPricePartI    = NULL
			SET @OrdIPricePartII   = NULL
			SET @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = NULL/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
			
			SET @OrdITracing = NULL /*RZ990818- Add ordITracing*/	
			/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
			SET @OrdIValidityExpireDate = NULL
			SET @OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian = NULL
			SET @OrdIValidityExpireDay = NULL
			SET @OrdIValidityExpireHour = NULL
			SET @OrdIValidityExpireMinute = NULL		
			/*RZ14010926- End*/

			/*NKR14020729- Start*/
			SET @OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin = NULL
			SET @OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryTermId = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryDate = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryDatePersian = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryTime = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryDay = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryEndDate = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryEndTime = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryEndDay = NULL
			SET @OrdIDeliveryUserId = NULL
			/*NKR14020729- End*/

			/**/
			IF(@OrdItem IS NOT NULL AND @OrdItem <> '')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @OrdItem = @OrdItem + '|'/*for last field*/
				DECLARE @PrdField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
				WHILE LEN(@OrdItem) > 0
				BEGIN
					IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@OrdItem) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @PrdField = SUBSTRING(@OrdItem, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @OrdItem))
						/**/
						DECLARE @PrdFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
						DECLARE @PrdFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
						SET @PrdFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PrdField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PrdField)-1)                                    
						SET @PrdFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PrdField, CHARINDEX('=', @PrdField)+1, Len(@PrdField))
						/**/
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPrdId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPrdId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/						
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPrdCode'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPrdCode = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIQuantity'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIQuantity = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPrice'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPrice = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdITotalPrice'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdITotalPrice = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDescription'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIQuantityDesc'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIQuantityDesc = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDiscountI'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDiscountI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDiscount'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDiscount = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDiscountPercent'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDiscountPercent = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDiscountIPercent'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDiscountIPercent = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPrdUnitId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPrdUnitId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdICurrencyUnitId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdICurrencyUnitId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIOrderView'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIOrderView = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIFCR'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIFCR = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdITax'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdITax = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdITaxIndex'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdITaxIndex = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdICalculateTax'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdICalculateTax = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@OrdICalculateTax AS BIT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdINotVisible'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdINotVisible = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS BIT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIFCRI'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIFCRI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdINotInSum'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdINotInSum = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS BIT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIRound'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIRound = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIRoundPlus'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIRoundPlus = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPricePartRate'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPricePartRate = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPricePartI'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPricePartI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPricePartII'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPricePartII = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END									  
						END		
						/*--------------------------*/	
						/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPrdPriceGroupID'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*RZ990206- End*/	
						/*--------------------------*/
						/*RZ990818- PordITracing*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdITracing'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdITracing = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END					
						/*--------------------------*/		
						/*RZ990818- End*/
						/*--------------------------*/
						/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIValidityExpireDate'
						BEGIN						
							SELECT @OrdIValidityExpireDate = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS DATETIME) END																			
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIValidityExpireDay'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIValidityExpireDay = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIValidityExpireHour'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIValidityExpireHour = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIValidityExpireMinute'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIValidityExpireMinute = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/	
						/*RZ14010926- End*/
						/*--------------------------*/
						/*NKR14020729- Start*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryTermId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryTermId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryDate'
						BEGIN						
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryDate = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS DATETIME) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryTime'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryTime = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryDay'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryDay = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryEndDate'
						BEGIN						
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryEndDate = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS DATETIME) END																			
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryEndTime'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryEndTime = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryEndDay'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryEndDay = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										THEN NULL 
										ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @PrdFieldName = 'OrdIDeliveryUserId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @OrdIDeliveryUserId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*NKR14020729- End*/
						/*--------------------------*/						
						/*--------------------------*/
						/*--------------------------*/
						SET @OrdItem = SUBSTRING(@OrdItem,
                                  LEN(@PrdField + '|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@OrdItem))
						/*--------------------------*/							
						/**/
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @PrdField = @OrdItem
						SET @OrdItem = NULL						
					END
				END
				/**/
				IF(@OrdIPrdCode IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIPrdCode <> '')
				BEGIN
					SET @OrdIPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [PrdCode] = @OrdIPrdCode)
				END
				ELSE IF (@OrdIPrdId IS NOT NULL AND @OrdIPrdId > 0)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @PrdId INT
					SET @PrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [ProductID] = @OrdIPrdId)
					IF(@PrdId IS NULL)
						SET @OrdIPrdId = NULL
				END
				/**/
				IF(@OrdIPrdId IS NULL OR @OrdIPrdId <= 0)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not Prd|'
				/**/
				/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup] WHERE [ProductPriceGroupID] = @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID) IS NULL)
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrdIPrdPriceGroupID|'
				END
				ELSE 
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
																FROM dbo.WSProductPrice PP
																WHERE PP.PrdId = @OrdIPrdId AND PP.Price = @OrdIPrice)
					IF(ISNULL(@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
													FROM dbo.WSProductPriceGroup PP
													ORDER BY PP.OrderView)													
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ990206- End*/
				/*=============== Discount =================*/
				/*RZ990206*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @OrdIDiscount = (ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
				END	
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIDiscountPercent,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @OrdIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0)
				END	
				/*RZ990206-END*/			
				/*=============== Calculate ================*/
				DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
				@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
				/*Tmp = Quantity * FCR * FCRI*//*FCRI =1 */
				SET @TmpI = @OrdIQuantity * ISNULL(@OrdIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@OrdIFCRI,1) /** 1 */
				/*TempII = price - discount - round + roundPlus*/				
				SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@OrdIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@OrdIRound,0) + ISNULL(@OrdIRoundPlus,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */
				/*=============== Tax =================*/
				/*RZ990818*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdITaxIndex,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @OrdITax = (ISNULL(@TmpII,0) * ISNULL(@OrdITaxIndex,0)) / 100
				END	
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdITaxIndex,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @OrdITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TmpII,0)
				END			
				/*RZ990818-End*/
				/*===================================*/
				/*TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * tax)*/
				SET @TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII)/* + (@TmpI * 0)*/
				IF(@OrdITotalPrice IS NULL )
					SET @OrdITotalPrice = @TotalPrice
				/**********************************************************/				
				/*Set OrderView*/
				IF(@OrdIOrderView IS NULL)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @ItemCount INT
					SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @InsertedOrderId)
					SET @OrdIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1		
				END
				/**********************************************************/
				/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
				DECLARE @OrdIPriceSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,
				@OrdIDiscountSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,			
				@OrdITaxSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0

				SET @OrdIPriceSum = (@TmpI * @TmpII)
				SET @OrdIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@OrdIDiscount,0) * @TmpI
				SET @OrdITaxSum = ISNULL(@OrdITax,0) * @TmpI
				/*RZ990818- End*/	
				/**********************************************************/
				/*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
				IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDate IS NULL)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDay IS NOT NULL OR @OrdIValidityExpireHour IS NOT NULL OR @OrdIValidityExpireMinute IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
					/**/						
						SET @OrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@OrdIValidityExpireDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
						SET @OrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@OrdIValidityExpireHour,0), @OrdIValidityExpireDate)
						SET @OrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@OrdIValidityExpireMinute,0), @OrdIValidityExpireDate)
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END							   
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF(@OrdIValidityExpireDate IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@OrdIValidityExpireDate AS DATE)))
				/**/
				END							
				/*RZ14010926- End*/
				/*NKR14020730- Start*/

				IF (@OrdIDeliveryDate IS NOT NULL)
					SET @OrdIDeliveryDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@OrdIDeliveryDate AS DATE)))

				IF (@OrdIDeliveryEndDate IS NOT NULL)
					SET @OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@OrdIDeliveryEndDate AS DATE)))

				/*NKR14020730- End*/
				/**********************************************************/


				/**/
				/*Main table*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
					   ([OrderId]
					   ,[ordIPrdId]
					   ,[ordIQuantity]
					   ,[ordIPrice]
					   ,[ordITotalPrice]
					   ,[ordIDescription]
					   ,[UserId]
					   ,[FPId]
					   ,[Month1]
					   ,[RegisterDate]
					   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
					   ,[ordIQuantityDesc]
					   ,[ordIDiscountI]
					   ,[ordIDiscount]
					   ,[ordIDiscountPercent]
					   ,[ordIDiscountIPercent]
					   ,[ordIPrdUnitId]
					   ,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
					   ,[ordIOrderView]
					   ,[ordIFCR]
					   ,[ordITax]
					   ,[ordITaxIndex]
					   ,[ordICalculateTax]
					   ,[ordINotVisible]
					   ,[ordIFCRI]
					   ,[ordINotInSum]
					   ,[ordIWHId]
					   ,[ordIRound]
					   ,[AppType]
					   ,[LanguageID]
					   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
					   ,[ordIRoundPlus]
					   ,[ordIPricePartRate]
					   ,[ordIPricePartI]
					   ,[ordIPricePartII]
					   ,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
					   /*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
					   ,[ordITracing]
					   ,[ordIPriceSum]
					   ,[ordIDiscountSum]				
					   ,[ordITaxSum]
					   /*RZ990818- End*/
					   /*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
					   ,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
					   ,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
					   /*RZ14010926- End*/
					   /*NKR14020729- Start*/
					   ,[ordIPrdValidityCountingMin]
					   ,[ordIPrdValidityCountingNum]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryTermId]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryDate]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryDatePersian]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryTime]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryDay]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryEndDate]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryEndTime]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryEndDay]
					   ,[ordIDeliveryUserId]
					   /*NKR14020729- End*/
					   )
				 VALUES
					   (@InsertedOrderId
					   ,@OrdIPrdId
					   ,@OrdIQuantity
					   ,@OrdIPrice
					   ,@OrdITotalPrice
					   ,@OrdIDescription
					   ,@UserId
					   ,@FPId
					   ,@Month1
					   ,@RegisterDate
					   ,@RegisterDatePersian
					   ,@OrdIQuantityDesc
					   ,@OrdIDiscountI
					   ,@OrdIDiscount
					   ,@OrdIDiscountPercent
					   ,@OrdIDiscountIPercent
					   ,@OrdIPrdUnitId
					   ,@OrdICurrencyUnitId
					   ,@OrdIOrderView
					   ,@OrdIFCR
					   ,@OrdITax
					   ,@OrdITaxIndex
					   ,@OrdICalculateTax
					   ,@OrdINotVisible
					   ,@OrdIFCRI
					   ,@OrdINotInSum
					   ,@WHId
					   ,@OrdIRound
					   ,@AppType
					   ,@DefaultLanguageID
					   ,@UserId
					   ,@RegisterDatePersian
					   ,@RegisterDate
					   ,@OrdIRoundPlus
					   ,@OrdIPricePartRate
					   ,@OrdIPricePartI
					   ,@OrdIPricePartII
					   ,@OrdIPrdPriceGroupID/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/					   
					   /*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
					   ,@OrdITracing
					   ,@OrdIPriceSum
					   ,@OrdIDiscountSum				
					   ,@OrdITaxSum
					   /*RZ990818- End*/
					   /*RZ14010926- Add OrdIValidityExpireDate*/
					   ,@OrdIValidityExpireDate
					   ,@OrdIValidityExpireDatePersian
					   /*RZ14010926- End*/
					   /*NKR14020729- Start*/
					   ,@OrdIPrdValidityCountingMin
					   ,@OrdIPrdValidityCountingNum
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryTermId
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryDate
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryDatePersian
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryTime
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryDay
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndDate
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndDatePersian
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndTime
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryEndDay
					   ,@OrdIDeliveryUserId
					   /*NKR14020729- End*/)
				/**/
				SET @InsertedOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			    /*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
					   ([OrderItemID]
					   ,[ordIDescription]
					   ,[ordIQuantityDesc]
					   ,[LanguageID])
				SELECT
					    @InsertedOrderItemId
					   ,@OrdIDescription
					   ,@OrdIQuantityDesc
					   ,LN.LanguageID
				FROM dbo.Languages LN 
				WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1	
				
				/**********************************************************/
				/*RZ14010919- Add PrdLinked and PrjPrdHis and ordCurrencyUnitId*/
				IF(ISNULL(@AddLinkedPrd,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertOrderItemFromLinkedPrd]
						@ProductId = @OrdIPrdId,
						@OrderId = @InsertedOrderId, 
						@ProductPriceGroupId = @OrdIPrdPriceGroupID,
						@OrdIWHId  = @WHId,
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@FPId = @FPId,
						@Month1 = @Month1,
						@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@AppType = @AppType,
						@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
						@LinkedPrdIds = NULL						
				/**/	
				END
				/*RZ14010919- End*/
				/**********************************************************/

			/**/
			END
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO  @OrdItem
		END
		CLOSE CrsrItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrItem
		/**/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Order Items' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 		
		/*============================ Order Payments ====================*/	
		IF(@PaymentJson	IS NOT NULL AND @PaymentJson <>'')
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @OrdPayTypeId INT,
					@OrdPayDocId INT,
					@OrdPayDocTypeId INT,
					@OrdPayAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
					@OrdPayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50),
					@OrdPayDocDate NVARCHAR(15),
					@OrdPayDescription NVARCHAR(2000),
					@OrdPayDocDateTime DATETIME,
					@OrdPayDocAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
					@PreOrderPaymentId INT,
					@OrdPay NVARCHAR(MAX),
					@OrdpayBankDocNumber NVARCHAR(50)/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
			/**/
			BEGIN TRAN
			DECLARE CrsrPay CURSOR FOR 		
				SELECT 
				(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
									FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@PaymentJson) PJIN
									WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@PaymentJson) PJ
				WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
			OPEN CrsrPay
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPay INTO @OrdPay
			/**/
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN		
			/**/
				DECLARE @InsertedOrdPayId INT
				/**/
				SET @OrdPayTypeId = NULL
				SET @OrdPayDocId = NULL
				SET @OrdPayDocTypeId = NULL
				SET @OrdPayAmount = NULL
				SET @OrdPayDocNumber = NULL
				SET @OrdPayDocDate = NULL
				SET @OrdPayDescription = NULL
				SET @OrdPayDocDateTime = NULL
				SET @OrdPayDocAmount  = NULL
				SET @PreOrderPaymentId  = NULL
				SET @OrdpayBankDocNumber = NULL/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
				/**/
				IF(@OrdPay IS NOT NULL AND @OrdPay <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @OrdPay = @OrdPay + '|'/*for last field*/
					DECLARE @PayField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					WHILE LEN(@OrdPay) > 0
					BEGIN
						IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@OrdPay) > 0)
						BEGIN
							SET @PayField = SUBSTRING(@OrdPay, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @OrdPay))						
							/**/
							DECLARE @PayFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
							DECLARE @PayFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
							SET @PayFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PayField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PayField)-1)                                    
							SET @PayFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PayField, CHARINDEX('=', @PayField)+1, Len(@PayField))
							/**/
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayTypeId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdPayTypeId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayDocId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdPayDocId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayDocTypeId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdPayDocTypeId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayAmount'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdPayAmount = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayDocNumber'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdPayDocNumber = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PayFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayDocDateTime'
							BEGIN						
								SELECT @OrdPayDocDateTime = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS DATETIME) END
								/**/
								IF(@OrdPayDocDateTime IS NULL)
									SET @OrdPayDocDateTime = @RegisterDate							
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayDescription'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdPayDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PayFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdPayDocAmount'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdPayDocAmount = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PreOrderPaymentId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PreOrderPaymentId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/		
							/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'OrdpayBankDocNumber'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @OrdpayBankDocNumber = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PayFieldValue END
							END
							/*RZ990206- END*/
							/*--------------------------*/						
							/*--------------------------*/
							SET @OrdPay = SUBSTRING(@OrdPay,
									  LEN(@PayField + '|') + 1,
									  LEN(@OrdPay))
							/*--------------------------*/								
							/*--------------------------*/
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @PayField = @OrdPay
							SET @OrdPay = NULL						
						END
					END
					/**/	
					/**********************************************************/
					IF(@OrdPayTypeId IS NULL)
						SET @OrdPayTypeId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderPaymentTypeId] FROM dbo.WSOrderPaymentTypes PT ORDER BY OrderView)
					/**/
					IF(@OrdPayDocDateTime IS NULL)
						SET @OrdPayDocDateTime = @RegisterDate
					/**/
					IF(@OrdPayDocDateTime IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @OrdPayDocDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDate](CAST(@OrdPayDocDateTime AS DATE)))
						IF(@OrdPayDocDate IS NOT NULL AND @OrdPayDocDate <> '')
							SET @OrdPayDocDate = REPLACE(@OrdPayDocDate,'/','')
					END
					/*OrdpayDocAmount*/
					IF(ISNULL(@OrdpayDocAmount,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @OrdpayDocAmount = @OrdpayAmount
					END

					/**/
					/*Main table*/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]
						   ([ImportDate]
						   ,[ordpayTypeId]
						   ,[ordpayOrderId]
						   ,[ordpayDocId]
						   ,[ordpayDocTypeId]
						   ,[ordpayAmount]
						   ,[ordpayDocNumber]
						   ,[ordpayDocDate]
						   ,[ordpayDescription]
						   ,[ordpayFirstRegisterUserId]
						   ,[ordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian]
						   ,[ordpayFirstRegisteDate]
						   ,[UserId]
						   ,[FPId]
						   ,[Month1]
						   ,[RegisterDate]
						   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
						   ,[ImportDateTime]
						   ,[ordpayDocDateTime]
						   ,[ordpayDocAmount]
						   ,[PreOrderPaymentId]
						   ,[AppType]
						   ,[LanguageID]
						   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
						   ,[ordpayBankDocNumber]/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/)
					 VALUES
						   (@RegisterDatePersian
						   ,@OrdPayTypeId
						   ,@InsertedOrderId
						   ,@OrdPayDocId
						   ,@OrdPayDocTypeId
						   ,@OrdPayAmount
						   ,@OrdPayDocNumber
						   ,@OrdPayDocDate
						   ,@OrdPayDescription
						   ,@UserId
						   ,@RegisterDatePersian
						   ,@RegisterDate
						   ,@UserId
						   ,@FPId
						   ,@Month1
						   ,@RegisterDate
						   ,@RegisterDatePersian
						   ,@RegisterDate
						   ,@OrdPayDocDateTime
						   ,@OrdPayDocAmount
						   ,@PreOrderPaymentId
						   ,@AppType
						   ,@DefaultLanguageID
						   ,@UserId
						   ,@RegisterDatePersian
						   ,@RegisterDate
						   ,@OrdpayBankDocNumber/*RZ990206- Add ordIPrdPriceGroupID and ordpayBankDocNumber*/)
					/**/
					SET @InsertedOrdPayId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
					/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderPayments_Lang]
						   ([OrderPaymentId]
						   ,[ordpayDescription]
						   ,[LanguageID])
					SELECT
							@InsertedOrdPayId
						   ,@OrdPayDescription				
						   ,LN.LanguageID
					FROM dbo.Languages LN 
					WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
					AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1				
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPay INTO @OrdPay
			END
			/**/
			CLOSE CrsrPay
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPay
			/**/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Order Payments' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
		END

		/**********************************************************/
		/*RZ14010919- Add PrdLinked and PrjPrdHis and ordCurrencyUnitId*/
		IF(ISNULL(@AppPrjPrdHis,0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@InsertedOrderId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertProjectProductHistoryByOrderId]
			@OrderId = @InsertedOrderId, 
			@UserId = @UserId,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
			@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			@AppType = @AppType	
		/**/	
		END
		/*RZ14010919- End*/
		/**********************************************************/
		/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] 	@InsertedOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID				
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedOrderId = 0
	 SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(@ErrorFrom IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorFrom <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApispIssuePreOrder]

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispIssuePreOrder]
/*
	exec ApispIssuePreOrder		 								
*/
(
/*Info for Login*/
@UserName NVARCHAR(50),
@Password NVARCHAR(50),
/*PreOrder*/
@PordTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@PordNumber BIGINT = NULL,
@PordDate NVARCHAR(8),
@PordDateTime DATETIME,
@PordDay NVARCHAR(50),
@PordTime NVARCHAR(50),
@PordSubject NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@PordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
@PordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@PordIsVerified BIT = NULL,
@PordVerifierId INT = NULL,
@PordVerifierRequestId INT = NULL,
@PordVerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordStatusId INT = 1,
@PordIsFixed BIT = NULL,
@PordGuarantyId INT = NULL,
@PordPayConditionId INT = NULL,
@PordDeliveryTimeId INT = NULL,
@PordCreditDurationId INT = NULL,
@PordSpecialWord NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordDescriptionI NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@PordDescriptionII NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@PordDescriptionId INT = NULL,
@PordPrintFormatId INT = NULL,
@PordRLogoId INT = NULL,
@PordLLogoId INT = NULL,
@PordSigningI NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordSigningII NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordSigningIII NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordAccountNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordAccountShabaNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordAccountNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordAccountShabaNumberII NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordAddress NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordTelephone NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordFax NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordWebSite NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordEmail NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordSignatureId INT = NULL,
@PordDiscountTitle NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
@PordReferUserId INT = NULL,
@PordRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
@PordcmpTitle NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordDeliveryDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordDeliveryDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@PordDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PreOrderOprId INT = NULL,
@PordVerificationWorkFlowId INT = NULL,
@PordExpireDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordExpireTime NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordExpireDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
/*Company AND Person AND Project*/
@CompanyId INT = NULL,
@CompanyNumber INT = NULL,
@CompanyCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@CompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
@ProjectId INT = NULL,
@ProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
/*Product*/
@ProductJson NVARCHAR(MAX),
/*ProductSpecification*/
@ProductSpecificationJson NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
/*Payment*/
@PaymentJson NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
/*Warehouse*/
@WHId INT = NULL,
/**/
@DefualtProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100),
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(50),
@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(50),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedPreOrderId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedPreOrderNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,

/*NKR14020729- Start*/
@PordDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
@PordDeliveryEndDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordDeliveryEndDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@PordDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PordDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@PordDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
/*NKR14020729- End*/

)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/************************************ WHId **************************************/
IF(@WHId IS NULL OR @WHId <= 0)
	SET @WHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 1)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT

/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*WHID*/
IF(@WHId IS NOT NULL AND @WHId > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @wId INT
	SET @wId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE [WareHouseID] = @WHId)
	IF(@wId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WhId|'
END
/*PordVerifierId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordVerifierId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @PordVerifierId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerifierId|'
END
/*PordVerifierRequestId */
IF(ISNULL(@PordVerifierRequestId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @PordVerifierRequestId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerifierRequestId|'
END
/*PordStatusId */
IF(ISNULL(@PordStatusId ,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderStatusId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderStatus] WHERE [PreOrderStatusId] = @PordStatusId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordStatusId|'
END
ELSE
	SET @PordStatusId = 1
/*PordGuarantyId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordGuarantyId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [GuarantyID] FROM [dbo].[WSGuaranty] WHERE [GuarantyID] = @PordGuarantyId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordGuarantyId|'
END
/*PordPayConditionId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordPayConditionId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PayConditionID] FROM [dbo].[WSPayCondition] WHERE [PayConditionID] = @PordPayConditionId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordPayConditionId|'
END
/*PordDeliveryTimeId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordDeliveryTimeId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [DeliveryTimeID] FROM [dbo].[WSDeliveryTime] WHERE [DeliveryTimeID] = @PordDeliveryTimeId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordDeliveryTimeId|'
END
/*PordCreditDurationId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordCreditDurationId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CreditDurationID] FROM [dbo].[WSCreditDuration] WHERE [CreditDurationID] = @PordCreditDurationId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordCreditDurationId|'
END
/*PordPrintFormatId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordPrintFormatId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PrintFormatID] FROM [dbo].[PrintFormat] WHERE [PrintFormatID] = @PordPrintFormatId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordPrintFormatId|'
END
/*PordRLogoId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordRLogoId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [LogoID] FROM [dbo].[WSLogo] WHERE [LogoID] = @PordRLogoId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordRLogoId|'
END
/*PordLLogoId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordLLogoId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [LogoID] FROM [dbo].[WSLogo] WHERE [LogoID] = @PordLLogoId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordLLogoId|'
END
/*PordSignatureId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordSignatureId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [SignatureID] FROM [dbo].[WSSignature] WHERE [SignatureID] = @PordSignatureId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordSignatureId|'
END
/*PordReferUserId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordReferUserId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @PordReferUserId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordReferUserId|'
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @PordReferUserId = @UserId
END
/*PreOrderOprId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderOprId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderOprID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderOpr] WHERE [PreOrderOprID] = @PreOrderOprId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PreOrderOprId|'
END
/*PordVerificationWorkFlowId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordVerificationWorkFlowId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [VerificationWorkFlowId] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlows] WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @PordVerificationWorkFlowId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerificationWorkFlowId|'
END
/*------------------------*/
/*COMPANYID*/
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@CompanyNumber,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpNumber] = @CompanyNumber)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@CompanyCode,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpCode] = @CompanyCode)
END
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
/*COMPANYPERSONID*/	
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrsId INT
	SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId)
	IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
	ELSE IF((ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0))
	BEGIN	
		SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId AND [prsCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
	END
	/**/	
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @CompanyPersonId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
			([prsCompanyId]
			,[prsLName]           
			,[UserId]
			,[Year1]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[ImportDate]
			,[ImportDateTime]
			,[prsFirstRegisterUserId]           
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]           
			,[FirstRegisterDate])
		VALUES
			(@CompanyId
			,N'��������'           
			,@UserId
			,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@UserId
			,@AppType
			,@DefaultLanguageID
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate)
	
		SET @CompanyPersonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*MultiLanguage*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
			   ([CompanyPersonID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[prsLName])
		SELECT
				@CompanyPersonId
			   ,L.LanguageID
			   ,N'��������'
		FROM dbo.Languages L
		WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1 
		AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
	/**/
	END		
/**/
END
/*PROJECTID*/		
IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrjId INT
	SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId)
	IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
	ELSE IF((ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0))
	BEGIN	
		SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId AND [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
	END
END

		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 		
		/*=============================== PreOrder Items ====================*/	
		DECLARE @ItemTmpTable TABLE (/*because preorder item may be has specification*/
			[ItemId] INT,
			[InsertedId] INT
		)

			/*=============================================================*/

			@PordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,

			BEGIN TRAN
			DECLARE CrsrItem CURSOR FOR 		
				SELECT 
				(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
									FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@ProductJson) PJIN
									WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@ProductJson) PJ
				WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
			OPEN CrsrItem
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO @OrdItem
			/**/
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN		
			/**/
				DECLARE @InsertedPreOrderItemId INT
				/**/
				SET @PordItemId = NULL
				SET @PordIPrdId = NULL
				SET @PordIPrdCode = NULL
				SET @PordIQuantity = NULL
				SET @PordIPrice = NULL
				SET @PordITotalPrice = NULL
				SET @PordIDescription = NULL
				SET @PordIQuantityDesc = NULL
				SET @PordIDiscount = NULL
				SET @PordIDiscountI = NULL
				SET @PordIDiscountPercent = NULL
				SET @PordIDiscountIPercent = NULL
				SET @PordIPrdUnitId = NULL
				SET @PordICurrencyUnitId = NULL
				SET @PordIOrderView = NULL
				SET @PordIFCR = NULL
				SET @PordITax = NULL
				SET @PordITaxIndex = NULL

				SET @PordINotInSum = NULL

				SET @PordIRound = NULL
				SET @PordIRoundPlus = NULL
				SET @PordIPrdPriceGroupId = NULL
				/*RZ990818- Add PordITracing*/
				SET @PordITracing = NULL				
				/*RZ990818- End*/
				/*NKR14020730- Start*/
				SET @PordIValidityExpireDate = NULL
				SET @PordIValidityExpireDatePersian = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryTermId = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryDate = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryDatePersian = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryTime = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryDay = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryEndDate = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryEndTime = NULL
				SET @PordIDeliveryEndDay = NULL
				/*NKR14020730- End*/
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdItem,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @OrdItem = @OrdItem + '|'/*for last field*/
					DECLARE @PrdField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					WHILE LEN(@OrdItem) > 0
					BEGIN
						IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@OrdItem) > 0)
						BEGIN
							SET @PrdField = SUBSTRING(@OrdItem, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @OrdItem))
							/**/
							DECLARE @PrdFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
							DECLARE @PrdFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
							SET @PrdFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PrdField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PrdField)-1)                                    
							SET @PrdFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PrdField, CHARINDEX('=', @PrdField)+1, Len(@PrdField))
							/**/
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordItemId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordItemId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIPrdId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIPrdId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/						
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIPrdCode'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIPrdCode = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIQuantity'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIQuantity = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIPrice'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIPrice = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordITotalPrice'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordITotalPrice = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIDescription'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIQuantityDesc'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIQuantityDesc = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIDiscount'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIDiscount = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIDiscountI'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIDiscountI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIDiscountPercent'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIDiscountPercent = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIDiscountIPercent'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIDiscountIPercent = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END

							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIPrdUnitId'

								SELECT @PordIPrdUnitId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordICurrencyUnitId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordICurrencyUnitId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIOrderView'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIOrderView = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIFCR'

							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordITaxIndex'
							BEGIN

							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordINotInSum'

							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIWHId'

										  THEN NULL

							END										
							/*--------------------------*/
							/*RZ990818- PordITracing*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordITracing'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordITracing = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL

							/*--------------------------*/		
							/*RZ990818- End*/
							/*--------------------------*/	
							/*NKR14020727- Start*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIValidityExpireDate'

											THEN NULL

								SELECT @PordIDeliveryEndDate = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
											THEN NULL 
											ELSE CAST(@PrdFieldValue AS DATETIME) END																			
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIDeliveryEndTime'
							BEGIN

											THEN NULL 
											ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PrdFieldName = 'PordIDeliveryEndDay'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordIDeliveryEndDay = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PrdFieldValue,'')  = '' 
											THEN NULL 
											ELSE @PrdFieldValue END
							END
							/*NKR14020727- End*/
							/*--------------------------*/
							SET @OrdItem = SUBSTRING(@OrdItem,
									  LEN(@PrdField + '|') + 1,
									  LEN(@OrdItem))
							/*--------------------------*/							
							/**/
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @PrdField = @OrdItem
							SET @OrdItem = NULL						
						END
					END
					/**/
					/*NKR14020730- Start*/

					IF (@PordIValidityExpireDate IS NOT NULL)
						SET @PordIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@PordIValidityExpireDate AS DATE)))

					IF (@PordIDeliveryDate IS NOT NULL)
						SET @PordIDeliveryDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@PordIDeliveryDate AS DATE)))

					IF (@PordIDeliveryEndDate IS NOT NULL)
						SET @PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@PordIDeliveryEndDate AS DATE)))

					/*NKR14020730- End*/
					/**/
					/*======================================================*/					
					EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItems]
						/*PreOrder*/
						@PreOrderId  = @InsertedPreOrderId,

						@PordIPrdUnitId = @PordIPrdUnitId,
						@PordICurrencyUnitId  = @PordICurrencyUnitId,
						@PordIOrderView = @PordIOrderView,
						@PordIFCR  = @PordIFCR,
						@PordITax  = @PordITax,
						@PordITaxIndex  = @PordITaxIndex,
						@PordICalculateTax  = @PordICalculateTax,
						@PordINotVisible  = @PordINotVisible,
						@PordIFCRI  = @PordIFCRI,
						@PordINotInSum  = @PordINotInSum,
						@PordIWHId  = @PordIWHId,
						@PordIRound  = @PordIRound,
						@PordIRoundPlus  = @PordIRoundPlus,
						@PordIPrdPriceGroupId  = @PordIPrdPriceGroupId,					
						@PordITracing = @PordITracing,/*RZ990818- Add PordITracing*/					
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@Month1 = @Month1,
						@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@AppType = @AppType,
						@LanguageID = @LanguageID, 
						/**/
						@InsertedPreOrderItemId = @InsertedPreOrderItemId OUTPUT,
						@ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage OUTPUT,
						/*NKR14020730- Start*/
						@PordIValidityExpireDate = @PordIValidityExpireDate,
						@PordIValidityExpireDatePersian = @PordIValidityExpireDatePersian,
						@PordIDeliveryTermId = @PordIDeliveryTermId,
						@PordIDeliveryDate = @PordIDeliveryDate,
						@PordIDeliveryDatePersian = @PordIDeliveryDatePersian,
						@PordIDeliveryTime = @PordIDeliveryTime,
						@PordIDeliveryDay = @PordIDeliveryDay,
						@PordIDeliveryEndDate = @PordIDeliveryEndDate,
						@PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian = @PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,
						@PordIDeliveryEndTime = @PordIDeliveryEndTime,
						@PordIDeliveryEndDay = @PordIDeliveryEndDay
						/*NKR14020730- End*/
					/*for insert preOrderItemSpecification*/
					INSERT INTO @ItemTmpTable(ItemId,InsertedId)
					VALUES(@PordItemId,@InsertedPreOrderItemId)								
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO  @OrdItem
			END
			CLOSE CrsrItem
			DEALLOCATE CrsrItem
			/**/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder Items' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
		/**/
		END
		/*=================== PreOrder Item Specificatuion ================*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '' AND ISNULL(@ProductSpecificationJson,'') <> '')	
		BEGIN
		/**/		
			/*=============================================================*/
			DECLARE @PreOrderItemId INT,
			@Specification NVARCHAR(300),
			@SpecificationI NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
			@SpecificationII NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
			@SpecItem NVARCHAR(MAX)
			/*=============================================================*/
			BEGIN TRAN
			DECLARE CrsrItemSpec CURSOR FOR 		
				SELECT 
				(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
									FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@ProductSpecificationJson) PJIN
									WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@ProductSpecificationJson) PJ
				WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
			OPEN CrsrItemSpec
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItemSpec INTO @SpecItem
			/**/
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN		
			/**/
				DECLARE @InsertedPreOrderItemSpcflId INT,
				@InsertedPordItemId INT
				/**/
				SET @PreOrderItemId = NULL
				SET @Specification = NULL
				SET @SpecificationI = NULL
				SET @SpecificationII = NULL
				
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@SpecItem,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @SpecItem = @SpecItem + '|'/*for last field*/
					DECLARE @SpecField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					WHILE LEN(@SpecItem) > 0
					BEGIN
						IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@SpecItem) > 0)
						BEGIN
							SET @SpecField = SUBSTRING(@SpecItem, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @SpecItem))
							/**/
							DECLARE @SpecFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
							DECLARE @SpecFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
							SET @SpecFieldName = SUBSTRING(@SpecField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@SpecField)-1)                                    
							SET @SpecFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@SpecField, CHARINDEX('=', @SpecField)+1, Len(@SpecField))
							/**/
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @SpecFieldName = 'PreOrderItemId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PreOrderItemId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@SpecFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@SpecFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/							
							IF @SpecFieldName = 'Specification'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @Specification = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@SpecFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @SpecFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @SpecFieldName = 'SpecificationI'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @SpecificationI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@SpecFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @SpecFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @SpecFieldName = 'SpecificationII'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @SpecificationII = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@SpecFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @SpecFieldValue END
							END												
							/*--------------------------*/
							/*--------------------------*/
							SET @SpecItem = SUBSTRING(@SpecItem,
									  LEN(@SpecField + '|') + 1,
									  LEN(@SpecItem))
							/*--------------------------*/							
							/**/
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @SpecField = @SpecItem
							SET @SpecItem = NULL						
						END
					END
					/**/
					/*======================================================*/	
					IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderItemId,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @InsertedPordItemId = (SELECT TOP 1 InsertedId FROM @ItemTmpTable WHERE ItemId = @PreOrderItemId)
						IF(ISNULL(@InsertedPordItemId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
						BEGIN
						/**/
							EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecification]
							@PreOrderItemId =@InsertedPordItemId,
							@Specification = @Specification,
							@SpecificationI  = @SpecificationI,
							@SpecificationII = @SpecificationII,	
							/**/
							@UserId = @UserId,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
							@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
							@AppType = @AppType,
							@LanguageID = @LanguageID, 
							/**/
							@InsertedPreOrderItemSpcflId = @InsertedPreOrderItemSpcflId OUTPUT,
							@ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage OUTPUT							
						/**/
						END
					/**/
					END													
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItemSpec INTO  @SpecItem
			END
			CLOSE CrsrItemSpec
			DEALLOCATE CrsrItemSpec
			/**/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder Item Specification' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
		/**/
		END
				
		/*============================ PreOrder Payments ====================*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@PaymentJson,'') <> '' AND  ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @PordpayTypeId INT,
			@PordpayDocId INT,
			@PordpayDocTypeId INT,
			@PordpayAmount NUMERIC(18, 2),
			@PordpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50),
			@PordpayDocDate NVARCHAR(8),
			@PordpayDocDateTime DATETIME,
			@PordpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18, 2),
			@PordpayDescription NVARCHAR(2000),
			@OrdPay NVARCHAR(MAX)		
			/**/
			BEGIN TRAN
			DECLARE CrsrPay CURSOR FOR 		
				SELECT 
				(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
									FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@PaymentJson) PJIN
									WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
									FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
				FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@PaymentJson) PJ
				WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
			OPEN CrsrPay
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPay INTO @OrdPay
			/**/
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN		
			/**/
				DECLARE @InsertedPordPayId INT
				/**/
				SET @PordpayTypeId = NULL
				SET @PordpayDocId = NULL
				SET @PordpayDocTypeId = NULL
				SET @PordpayAmount = NULL
				SET @PordpayDocNumber = NULL
				SET @PordpayDocDate = NULL
				SET @PordpayDocDateTime = NULL
				SET @PordpayDocAmount = NULL
				SET @PordpayDescription = NULL
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdPay,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @OrdPay = @OrdPay + '|'/*for last field*/
					DECLARE @PayField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
					WHILE LEN(@OrdPay) > 0
					BEGIN
						IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@OrdPay) > 0)
						BEGIN
							SET @PayField = SUBSTRING(@OrdPay, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @OrdPay))						
							/**/
							DECLARE @PayFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
							DECLARE @PayFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
							SET @PayFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PayField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PayField)-1)                                    
							SET @PayFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PayField, CHARINDEX('=', @PayField)+1, Len(@PayField))
							/**/
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayTypeId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordpayTypeId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayDocId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordpayDocId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayDocTypeId'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordpayDocTypeId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS INT) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayAmount'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordpayAmount = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayDocNumber'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordpayDocNumber = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PayFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayDocDateTime'
							BEGIN						
								SELECT @PordpayDocDateTime = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS DATETIME) END
								/**/
								IF(@PordpayDocDateTime IS NULL)
									SET @PordpayDocDateTime = @RegisterDate							
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayDescription'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordpayDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'')  = '' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE @PayFieldValue END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/
							IF @PayFieldName = 'PordpayDocAmount'
							BEGIN
								SELECT @PordpayDocAmount = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@PayFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
										  THEN NULL 
										  ELSE CAST(@PayFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,2)) END
							END
							/*--------------------------*/							
							/*--------------------------*/
							SET @OrdPay = SUBSTRING(@OrdPay,
									  LEN(@PayField + '|') + 1,
									  LEN(@OrdPay))
							/*--------------------------*/								
							/*--------------------------*/
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @PayField = @OrdPay
							SET @OrdPay = NULL						
						END
					END
					/**/	
					/**********************************************************/					
					IF(@PordpayDocDateTime IS NULL)
						SET @PordpayDocDateTime = @RegisterDate
					/**/
					IF(@PordpayDocDateTime IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @PordpayDocDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@PordpayDocDateTime AS DATE)))						
					END
					/*========================================================*/
					EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderPayments] 								
					@PreOrderId = @InsertedPreOrderId,
					@PordNumber = NULL,
					/*PreOrder Payment*/
					@PordpayTypeId = @PordpayTypeId,
					@PordpayDocId = @PordpayDocId,
					@PordpayDocTypeId = @PordpayDocTypeId,
					@PordpayAmount = @PordpayAmount,
					@PordpayDocNumber = @PordpayDocNumber,
					@PordpayDocDate = @PordpayDocDate,
					@PordpayDocDateTime = @PordpayDocDateTime,
					@PordpayDocAmount = @PordpayDocAmount,
					@PordpayDescription = @PordpayDescription,
					/**/
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@Month1 = @Month1,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@AppType = @AppType,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID, 
					/**/
					@InsertedPreOrderPaymentId = @InsertedPordPayId OUTPUT,
					@ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage OUTPUT		
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPay INTO @OrdPay
			END
			/**/
			CLOSE CrsrPay
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPay
			/**/
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Order Payments' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
		END

		/*=========================== Calculate PreOrder ====================*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')		
			EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] 	@InsertedPreOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID	
		/**/
	END
	/*=========================================================================*/		
	IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1					
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
		SET @InsertedPreOrderNumber = 0
		SELECT 0			
	/**/
	END	
END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
	 SET @InsertedPreOrderNumber = 0
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(ISNULL(@ErrorFrom,'') <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo]






/*RZ14010714*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo]			
(
/* 
	exec ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo 
	@UpdateDetail = N'CrmCompanyJustAccessCity',
	@UserId = 3,
	@RollId = NULL
*/	
@UpdateDetail NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@UserId INT = NULL,
@RollId INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/		
	IF(@UpdateDetail IS NULL OR @UpdateDetail = '')
		SET @UpdateDetail = ISNULL(@UpdateDetail,'') 
	/**/
	IF(@UserId IS NOT NULL AND @UserId <> 0)
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
		SET 
			[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdate] = 1
			,[MobAppBaseInfoLastUpdate] = GETDATE()
			,[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail] = (SELECT (
												  CASE 
													WHEN 
														((SELECT TOP 1 splitdata
														FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],',')
														WHERE splitdata = @UpdateDetail) IS NULL)						
													 THEN ISNULL([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],'') + @UpdateDetail + ','  
													 ELSE [MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail]  
												  END  
											  ))
		WHERE [UserId] = @UserId

	END
	ELSE IF (@RollId IS NOT NULL AND @RollId <> 0)
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
		SET 
			[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdate] = 1
			,[MobAppBaseInfoLastUpdate] = GETDATE() 
			,[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail] = (SELECT (
												  CASE 
													WHEN 
														((SELECT TOP 1 splitdata
														FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],',')
														WHERE splitdata = @UpdateDetail) IS NULL)						
													 THEN ISNULL([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],'') + @UpdateDetail + ','  
													 ELSE [MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail]  
												  END  
											  ))
		WHERE [UserId] IN (SELECT [UserId] FROM [dbo].[UsersPermission] WHERE [RollId] = @RollId)

	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
		SET 
			[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdate] = 1
			,[MobAppBaseInfoLastUpdate] = GETDATE() 
			,[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail] = (SELECT (
												  CASE 
													WHEN 
														((SELECT TOP 1 splitdata
														FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],',')
														WHERE splitdata = @UpdateDetail) IS NULL)						
													 THEN ISNULL([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],'') + @UpdateDetail + ','  
													 ELSE [MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail]  
												  END  
											  ))
	END
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispOrderVerify]





/*NKR14020802*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[ApispOrderVerify]
(
	/*
		EXEC [ApispOrderVerify]
	*/
	@OrderId INT,
	@VerifierUserId INT,
	@VerifyDate DATETIME,
	@DefLangCollation NVARCHAR(50) = 'fa-IR'
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	UPDATE dbo.WSOrder
	   SET ordIsVerified = 1,
		   ordVerifierId = @VerifierUserId,
		   ordIsFixed = 1,
		   ordVerifiedRegisterDate = @VerifyDate,
		   ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = FORMAT(@VerifyDate, 'yyyyMMdd', @DefLangCollation)
	 WHERE OrderID = @OrderId
	/**/
END

	exec ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView 
	@CompanyId  = 6156,

/**/
END
