-- [dbo].[ApispTransferUsersToAnotherPosition]








/*RZ14010714*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispTransferUsersToAnotherPosition]
/*
	exec ApispTransferUsersToAnotherPosition 2,3,'60,54,58,',3,'2020-09-27','13990706',7,'dasti'	
*/
(
@OldPositionId INT,
@PositionId INT,
@CrmUserIds NVARCHAR(2000),
/**/
@UserId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@Month INT,
@AppType NVARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*================= Create tmpTable for get child a position =================*/
DECLARE @PositionTmpTable TABLE (
	[PositionId] INT
)
/*============================================================================*/
	/*===================== Delete LinkedUsers ================*/		
	DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmLinkedUsers]
	WHERE  [CrmUserId] IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,'0') AS INT) FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@CrmUserIds,','))
	AND [LinkerPart] = 2/*RZ990716- Add LinkerPart = 2*/	
	/*============ Delete LinkedUsers on OdlLink From parent =============*/	
	/*======================= Get Parent =================================*/
	DELETE FROM @PositionTmpTable			
	;WITH Rollups AS (
		SELECT [PositionId], ParentId
		FROM [UserPositions] Child WHERE [PositionId] in (@OldPositionId)
		UNION ALL
		SELECT cl.[PositionId], cl.ParentId
		FROM [UserPositions] cl 
		INNER JOIN Rollups Children ON Children.ParentID = cl.[PositionId]
	)
			
	INSERT INTO @PositionTmpTable 
		([PositionId])
	SELECT DISTINCT
		Rollups.[PositionId]
	FROM Rollups;	
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 * FROM @PositionTmpTable) IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmLinkedUsers]				
		WHERE  LinkedUserId IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,'0') AS INT) FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@CrmUserIds,','))
		AND [CrmUserId] IN (SELECT
								U.UserId					
							FROM dbo.[Users] U 
							WHERE U.[PositionId]  IN (SELECT LP.[LinkedPositionId] 
													FROM [dbo].[CrmLinkedUserPositions] LP 
													WHERE LP.[LinkedPositionId] IN(SELECT [PositionId] FROM @PositionTmpTable)))
		AND [LinkerPart] = 2		
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	/*====================================================================*/

	UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
	SET 
		 [UserId1] = @UserId
		,[Month1] = @Month
		,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
		,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian        
		,[AppType] = @AppType
		,[PositionId] = @PositionId
	WHERE [PositionId] = @OldPositionId
	AND UserId IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,'0') AS INT) FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@CrmUserIds,','))	
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApispVerificationRequestVerify]






/*NKR14020803*/
/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
/*RZ14030308- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to letter*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispVerificationRequestVerify]
(		
/* 
	EXEC [ApispVerificationRequestVerify]
*/
@VerificationWorkFlowId INT,
@ObjectId INT,
@VerificationRequestId INT,
@VerificationWorkFlowStepId INT,
@VerifyText NVARCHAR(MAX),
@VerifyTextLang NVARCHAR(MAX),
@MsgSubject NVARCHAR(200),
@VerifiedMsgSubject NVARCHAR(200),
@RollId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@MaxDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT,
@Result NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @FPId INT, @Year1 SMALLINT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
SET @Year1 = @FPId
/*===================================== Define variables ==================================*/
DECLARE @ObjectType INT, @SequenceObservation BIT,
@LimitPreOrderDiscountActive BIT,@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),
@LimitOrderDiscountActive BIT,@LimitOrderDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),
@DiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
@SumDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
@MsgTypeId INT,@VerifyUserId INT, @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),@InternalMessageId INT
/*RZ14001018- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
,@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2)
/*RZ14001018- End*/
SET @RegisterTime = CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME)
/*============================= SET Object and @SequenceObservation =======================*/	
SELECT @ObjectType = [ObjectType], @SequenceObservation = [SequenceObservation]
FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlows]
WHERE VerificationWorkFlowId = @VerificationWorkFlowId
/*================================= Check Discount Percent ================================*/
SELECT @LimitPreOrderDiscountActive = LimitPreOrderDiscountActive, 
@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent = LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent,
@LimitOrderDiscountActive = LimitOrderDiscountActive,
@LimitOrderDiscountPercent = LimitOrderDiscountPercent
FROM dbo.CrmsysRolls
WHERE ID = @RollId

IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 10)/*PreOrder*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@LimitPreOrderDiscountActive,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @MaxDiscountPercent = ISNULL(@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent,0)
	END
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @pordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2)
		SET @pordDiscount = (SELECT pordDiscount FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = @ObjectId)
		SELECT 
			@SumPriceAmount = SumPriceAmount,
			@SumDiscount = SumDiscount, 
			/*RZ14001018- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
			@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount = TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount ,
			@TotalDiscountSum = TotalDiscountSum
			/*RZ14001018- End*/
		FROM [dbo].[WSfnReturnPreOrderSumFooter](@ObjectId)				

		/**/						
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		/*IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0)		
			SET @Result = '-1' /-*DivisionByZero*-/
		ELSE*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0 )	
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)

			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
			/**/					
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')	/*RZ14000202- Modify for DivisionByZero*//*RZ14001018- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@SumDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)*//*calculate product discount*/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)/*RZ14001018- Modify Calculate discount in item*/

			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
			/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)/*calculate preorder discount*/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
				SET @Result = '-3' /*The pre-order discount is too high*/	
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) <> 0)	
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-1' /*DivisionByZero*/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/

	END
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 20)/*Order*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@LimitOrderDiscountActive,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @MaxDiscountPercent = ISNULL(@LimitOrderDiscountPercent,0)
	END
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @ordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2)
		SET @ordDiscount = (SELECT ordDiscount FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @ObjectId)
		SELECT 
			@SumPriceAmount = SumPriceAmount,
			@SumDiscount = SumDiscount,
			/*RZ14001018- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
			@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount = TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount ,
			@TotalDiscountSum = TotalDiscountSum
			/*RZ14001018- End*/
		FROM [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderSumFooter](@ObjectId)
		/**/				
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		/*IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0)		
			SET @Result = '-1' /-*DivisionByZero*-/
		ELSE*/
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0 )	
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')	/*RZ14000202- Modify for DivisionByZero*//*RZ14001018- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
		BEGIN
			/*SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@SumDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)*//*calculate product discount*/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)/*RZ14001018- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
			/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)/*calculate order discount*/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
				SET @Result = '-3' /*The order discount is too high*/	
		END
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0) <> 0)	
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-1' /*DivisionByZero*/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/
	END
/**/
END
/*RZ14001128- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to dyform*/
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 80)/*DyForm*/
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @DyWorkflowId INT = 0;
	SET @DyWorkflowId = (SELECT TOP (1) [WorkflowId]
						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
						WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @ObjectId)

	IF(ISNULL(@DyWorkflowId,0) <> 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/	
		IF(EXISTS (SELECT [FormId] 
					FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] VMD 
					WHERE VMD.[MasterValueId] = @ObjectId
					AND  ISNULL(VMD.[IsChecked],0) = 0
					AND VMD.FormId <> (SELECT MV.FormId FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] MV WHERE MV.FormFieldMasterValueId = @ObjectId)))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-6' /*the mastervalue hav masterValueDetails in workflow not checked*/	
		/**/	
		END
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*RZ14001128- End*/



/*================================== Check steps before current step ======================*/
IF(ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @StepBfCurrId INT
	SET @StepBfCurrId = (SELECT TOP 1 [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]       	   
							FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
							WHERE VerificationWorkFlowId = @VerificationWorkFlowId
							AND ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]) < (SELECT ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])  
																						FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWSI 
																						WHERE VWSI.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = @VerificationWorkFlowStepId)
							AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
																	 WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)	
							ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]))
	IF(ISNULL(@StepBfCurrId,0) > 0 )
	BEGIN
		IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-4|' + ISNULL((SELECT [VerificationWorkFlowStepTitle]  	   
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
											WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = @StepBfCurrId),'')
		END
		ELSE IF(@LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-4|' + ISNULL((SELECT [VerificationWorkFlowStepTitle] 	   
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps_Lang] VWS
											WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = @StepBfCurrId AND VWS.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')	
		END

	END
/**/
END

DECLARE @CompanyId INT = NULL, @cmpReferUserId INT = NULL, @cmpSuppUserId INT = NULL

/*----------------------*/

		@CompanyId = pordCompanyId
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
	WHERE PreOrderID = @ObjectId	
/**/
END

BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT TOP(1) 
		@CompanyId = ordCompanyId
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
	WHERE OrderID = @ObjectId	
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 40) /*bord*/
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT TOP(1) 
		@CompanyId = bordCompanyId
	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
	WHERE BuyOrderID = @ObjectId

END

/**/

		@CompanyId = stsupCompanyId
	FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
	WHERE StockSupplyId = @ObjectId	
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 60) /*doc*/

/**/

		@CompanyId = CompanyId
	FROM [dbo].[WSDoc]
	WHERE DocID = @ObjectId	
/**/

BEGIN
/**/

	FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
	WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @ObjectId	
/**/
END

BEGIN

	WHERE [LeaveId] = @ObjectId

/*RZ14030308- End*/

IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) <> 0)

/**/

/*RZ14011210- Add responsible user*/
DECLARE @ResponsibleUserId INT
DECLARE @VerificationReqUserIdI INT
SELECT @VerificationReqUserIdI = [VerificationReqUserId] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] WHERE [VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId
IF(ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 1)
BEGIN
/**/	
	SELECT TOP 1
		 @VerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]	
	FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
	WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
												WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] <> @VerificationWorkFlowStepId
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])			
	IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -101)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ResponsibleUserId = (SELECT TOP(1) [ResponsibleUserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = @VerificationReqUserIdI)
		IF(ISNULL(@ResponsibleUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-101' /*Not Set Responsible*/
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14020607- Add cmpReferUserId and cmpSuppUserId to verifyUser*/
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -102 AND ISNULL(@cmpReferUserId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Result = '-102' /*Not Set cmpReferUserId*/
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -103 AND ISNULL(@cmpSuppUserId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Result = '-103' /*Not Set cmpSuppUserId*/
	/**/
	END
	/*RZ14020607- End*/
/**/
END IF(ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT TOP 1
		 @VerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]	
	FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
	WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
												WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] <> @VerificationWorkFlowStepId
	AND [VerifyUserId] = -101
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])

	IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -101)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ResponsibleUserId = (SELECT TOP(1) [ResponsibleUserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = @VerificationReqUserIdI)
		IF(ISNULL(@ResponsibleUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-101' /*Not Set Responsible*/
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END	
	
	/*RZ14020607- Add cmpReferUserId and cmpSuppUserId to verifyUser*/
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @VerifyUserId = NULL
		SELECT TOP(1)
			@VerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]
		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
		WHERE VerificationWorkFlowId = @VerificationWorkFlowId	
		AND [VerifyUserId] = -102
		ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])

		IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -102 AND ISNULL(@cmpReferUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-102' /*Not Set cmpReferUserId*/
		/**/
		END		
	/**/
	END
	
	/*--------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @VerifyUserId = NULL
		SELECT TOP(1)
			@VerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]
		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
		WHERE VerificationWorkFlowId = @VerificationWorkFlowId	
		AND [VerifyUserId] = -103
		ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])
		IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -103 AND ISNULL(@cmpSuppUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-103' /*Not Set cmpSuppUserId*/
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14020607- End*/	
/**/
END
SET @VerifyUserId = NULL
	
/*RZ14011210- End*/
/*===================================== Verify Request =====================================*/
IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*======================== Create Tmp table for language ==========================*/
	/*Sample= @VerifyTextLang = N'[LangId=1033|,|Txt=TextI|,|Subj=subjectI|,|VerifiedSubj=vSubjI|,|]@[LangId=1025|,|Txt=TextII|,|Subj=subjectII|,|VerifiedSubj=vSubjII|,|]@',*/
	DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
		[LangId] INT,
		[Txt] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[Subj] NVARCHAR(200),
		[VerifiedSubj] NVARCHAR(200)
	)
	DECLARE @LangId INT,@Txt NVARCHAR(MAX), @Subj NVARCHAR(200),@Text NVARCHAR(MAX),@VerifiedSubj NVARCHAR(200)
	IF(ISNULL(@VerifyTextLang,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
		/**/	
		DECLARE Crsr Cursor For
		/**/	
			SELECT SplitData  
			FROM fnSplitString(@VerifyTextLang, '@')
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @Text
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*================================================================================*/
			DECLARE @Field NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
			SET @LangId = NULL
			SET @Txt = NULL
			SET @Subj = NULL
			SET @VerifiedSubj = NULL
			/**/
			/*****************************************************************/
			IF(ISNULL(@Text,'') <> '') 
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Text = SUBSTRING(@Text, 2, LEN(@Text)-2)			
				/*===================== Get Fields ==========================*/
				WHILE LEN(@Text) > 0
				BEGIN
					IF PATINDEX('%|,|%', @Text) > 0
					BEGIN
						SET @Field = SUBSTRING(@Text, 0, PATINDEX('%|,|%', @Text))
						/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
						/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
						/*For example => LangId=1065 => LangId= => LangId => Compare => value*/
						DECLARE @FieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
						DECLARE @FieldName NVARCHAR(200)
						SET @FieldName = SUBSTRING(@Field, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@Field)-1)                                    
						SET @FieldValue = SUBSTRING(@Field, CHARINDEX('=', @Field)+1, Len(@Field))
						/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
						/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
						/*BEGIN*/
						IF @FieldName = 'LangId'
						BEGIN
							SET @LangId = CASE WHEN CAST(ISNULL(@FieldValue,'0') AS INT) = 0 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@FieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @FieldName = 'Txt'
						BEGIN
							SET @Txt = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @FieldName = 'Subj'
						BEGIN
							SET @Subj = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @FieldName = 'VerifiedSubj'
						BEGIN
							SET @VerifiedSubj = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/				
						/*END */
						/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
						/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
						SET @Text = SUBSTRING(@Text,LEN(@Field + '|,|') + 1,LEN(@Text))
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Field = @Text
						SET @Text = NULL       
					END
				END
				/*===========================================================*/
				INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([LangId],Txt,Subj,VerifiedSubj)
				VALUES(@LangId,@Txt,@Subj,@VerifiedSubj)
			/**/
			END
			/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @Text
		END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr
	/**/
	END		
	/*============================= Insert into WSVerificationRequestDetails ===================*/	
	DECLARE @VerificationRequestDetailId INT
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
           ([VerificationRequestId]
           ,[ObjectId]
           ,[ObjectType]
           ,[VerificationWorkFlowId]
           ,[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]
           ,[VerifyUserId]
           ,[VerifyDateTime]
           ,[VerifyText]
           ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[FirstRegisterDate]
           ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
           ,[UserId]
           ,[Year1]
           ,[Month1]
           ,[RegisterDate]
           ,[RegisterDatePersian]
           ,[AppType]
           ,[LanguageID])
    VALUES
           (@VerificationRequestId
           ,@ObjectId
           ,@ObjectType
           ,@VerificationWorkFlowId
           ,@VerificationWorkFlowStepId
           ,@UserId
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@VerifyText
           ,@UserId
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@UserId
           ,@FPId
           ,@Month1
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@AppType
           ,@DefaultLanguageID)
	SET @VerificationRequestDetailId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	IF(ISNULL(@VerificationRequestDetailId,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*Insert into lang tabel*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
           ([VerificationRequestDetailId]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[VerifyText])
		SELECT
            @VerificationRequestDetailId
           ,[LangId]
           ,[Txt]
		FROM @TmpTable
		WHERE [LangId] <> 0
		/**/
		/*============================= IsFixed Object =======================*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*Add (@ObjectType = 30) ==> BuyOrder,(@ObjectType = 40) ==> Do */
			IF(@ObjectType = 10)/*PreOrder*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
					SET [pordIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE PreOrderID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 5
			END
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 20)/*Order*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
					SET [ordIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE OrderID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 8
			END
			/*RZ14030308- Add WSStockSupply and buyOrder*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 40)/*BuyOrder*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
					SET [bordIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE BuyOrderID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 9	
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 45)/*StockSupply*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
					SET [stsupIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE StockSupplyId = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 21	
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14030308- End*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 80)/*DyForm*//*RZ14001128- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to dyform*/
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SET @MsgTypeId = 16
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14010929- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to doc*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 60)/*Doc*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSDoc]
					SET [IsFix] = 1      
				WHERE DocID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 20	
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14010929- End*/
			/*AI14011113*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 90)/*leave*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[CrmLeave]
				   SET [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId      
				WHERE [LeaveId] = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 6
			END
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 100)/*mission*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[CrmLeave]
				   SET [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId      
				WHERE [LeaveId] = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 7
			END
			/*AI14011113-End*/
			/*RZ14030308- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to letter*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 110) /*Letter*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*UPDATE [dbo].[SecLetters]
					SET [IsFix] = 1      
				WHERE LetterId = @ObjectId*/
				SET @MsgTypeId = 14	
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14030308- End*/
		END
		/*============================= Find next step =======================*/	
		DECLARE @NextStepId INT,@NextStepVerifyUserId INT
		SELECT TOP 1
			 @NextStepId = [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]
			/*,@NextStepVerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]	*/			
			/*RZ14011210- Add responsible user*/
			,@NextStepVerifyUserId = (CASE WHEN ISNULL([VerifyUserId],0) = -101
										THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [ResponsibleUserId] 
												FROM [dbo].[Users] U
												WHERE U.UserId = @VerificationReqUserIdI										   
											)
										/*RZ14020607- Add cmpReferUserId and cmpSuppUserId to verifyUser*/
										WHEN ISNULL([VerifyUserId],0) = -102
										THEN (@cmpReferUserId)
										WHEN ISNULL([VerifyUserId],0) = -103
										THEN (@cmpSuppUserId)
										/*RZ14020607- End*/
										ELSE [VerifyUserId]
									END)
			/*RZ14011210- End*/
		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
		WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId
		AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
												 FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
												 WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)
		ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])			
		/*======================= GET VerificationReqUserId ==================*/
		DECLARE @VerificationReqUserId INT
		SELECT @VerificationReqUserId = [VerificationReqUserId] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] WHERE [VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId
		/*============================= Send Message =======================*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@NextStepId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInternalMessageLoadReceiver] @crmUserId = @VerificationReqUserId
			EXEC [dbo].[CRMspIntMsgUpdateSelectReceiver]
						@crmUserId = @VerificationReqUserId,@UserId = @NextStepVerifyUserId,@Selected = 1, @Mode  = 1  */

			/*AI14011223 - instead of [CRMspInternalMessageSend]*/
			EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInternalMessageSendSingle]
						@msgMasterId = NULL,
						@msgSubject = @MsgSubject,
						@msgBody = @VerifyText,
						/*@msgActiveInCycle = NULL, AI14011223*/
						@msgdTypeId = @MsgTypeId,
						@msgdFromUserId = @VerificationReqUserId,
						@msgdToUserId = @NextStepVerifyUserId,
						@msgdSendDate=@RegisterDatePersian,
						@msgdSendTime = @RegisterTime,
						@msgdSendDateTime = @RegisterDate,
						@msgdPriorityId = NULL,
						@msgdAttachFile = NULL,
						@msgdAttachRecId = @ObjectId,
						@msgdAttachRecDesc = NULL,
						@msgdGroupId  = NULL,
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@Year1 = @Year1,
						@Month1 = @Month1,
						@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@AppType = @AppType, 
						@InsertedID = @InternalMessageId OUTPUT, /*AI14011223 - instead of @InternalMessageID*/
						@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
						@msgdAttachRecIdI = @NextStepId,
						@msgdAttachRecDescI = NULL

			/*Insert Lang table*/
			IF(ISNULL(@InternalMessageId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmInternalMessage_Lang]
					([InternalMessageID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[msgSubject]
					,[msgBody])
				SELECT
					@InternalMessageId
					,[LangId]
					,Subj
					,Txt
				FROM @TmpTable 
				WHERE [LangId] <> 0 
			END			
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@NextStepId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*============================= Verify Object =============================*/	
			IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*Add (@ObjectType = 30) ==> BuyOrder,(@ObjectType = 40) ==> Do */
				IF(@ObjectType = 10)/*PreOrder*/
				BEGIN
					EXEC dbo.WSspPreOrderVerify @ObjectId,@UserId,@RegisterDatePersian
				END
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 20)/*Order*/
				BEGIN
					EXEC dbo.WSspOrderVerify @ObjectId,@UserId,@RegisterDatePersian
				END
				/*RZ14030308- Add WSStockSupply and buyOrder*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 40)/*BuyOrder*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.WSspBuyOrderVerify
					@BuyOrderId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 45)/*Stocksupply*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.WSspStockSupplyVerify
					@StockSupplyId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14030308- End*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 80)/*DyForm*//*RZ14001128- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to dyform*/
				BEGIN
				/**/

					DECLARE @DyFrmWorkflowId INT = 0;
					SET @DyFrmWorkflowId = (SELECT TOP (1) [WorkflowId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @ObjectId)

					IF(ISNULL(@DyFrmWorkflowId,0) = 0)/*normal form*/
					BEGIN
					/**/	
						EXEC dbo.CRMspDyFormCheck 
						@MasterValueId = @ObjectId,
						@CheckerUserId = @UserId,
						@CheckedDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@Mode = 1
					/**/
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
					/**/	
						EXEC dbo.CRMspDyFormCheck 
						@MasterValueId = @ObjectId,
						@CheckerUserId = @UserId,
						@CheckedDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@Mode = 2
					/**/
					END					
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14010929- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to doc*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 60)/*Doc*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.WSspDocVerify
					@DocId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14010929- End*/
				/*AI14011113*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 90)/*leave*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.CRMspLeaveVerify
					@LeaveId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 100)/*mission*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.CRMspLeaveVerify
					@LeaveId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				/*AI14011113-End*/
				/*RZ14030308- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to letter*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 110) /*Letter*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC [dbo].[SecspLetterVerify]
					@LetterId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,					
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@AppType = @AppType					
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14030308- End*/
			END
			/*======================== Expired All Request but current request ========*/
			EXEC WSspUpdateVerificationRequestsExpired 
				@VerificationRequestId = @VerificationRequestId,
				@VerificationWorkFlowId = 0,
				@ObjectId = @ObjectId,
				@ObjectType = @ObjectType,
				@Expired = 1,
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
			/*================== Send Message for the object is verified ==============*/
			/*EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInternalMessageLoadReceiver] @crmUserId = @UserId
			EXEC [dbo].[CRMspIntMsgUpdateSelectReceiver]
				@crmUserId = @UserId,@UserId = @VerificationReqUserId,@Selected = 1, @Mode  = 1 AI14011223 */

			/*AI14011223 - instead of [CRMspInternalMessageSend]*/
			EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInternalMessageSendSingle]
				@msgMasterId = NULL,
				@msgSubject = @VerifiedMsgSubject,
				@msgBody = @VerifyText,
				/*@msgActiveInCycle = NULL, AI14011223*/
				@msgdTypeId = @MsgTypeId,
				@msgdFromUserId = @UserId,
				@msgdToUserId = @VerificationReqUserId,
				@msgdSendDate=@RegisterDatePersian,
				@msgdSendTime = @RegisterTime,
				@msgdSendDateTime = @RegisterDate,
				@msgdPriorityId = NULL,
				@msgdAttachFile = NULL,
				@msgdAttachRecId = @ObjectId,
				@msgdAttachRecDesc = NULL,
				@msgdGroupId  = NULL,
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@Year1 = @Year1,
				@Month1 = @Month1,
				@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
				@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
				@AppType = @AppType, 
				@InsertedID = @InternalMessageId OUTPUT, /*AI14011223 - instead of @InternalMessageID*/
				@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
				@msgdAttachRecIdI = NULL,
				@msgdAttachRecDescI = NULL
			/*Insert Lang table*/
			IF(ISNULL(@InternalMessageId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmInternalMessage_Lang]
					([InternalMessageID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[msgSubject]
					,[msgBody])
				SELECT
					@InternalMessageId
					,[LangId]
					,[VerifiedSubj]
					,[Txt]
				FROM @TmpTable 
				WHERE [LangId] <> 0 
			END			
			/*=========================================================================*/
		/**/
		END		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE
		SET @Result = '-5'/*error in insert into WSVerificationRequestDetails*/
/**/
END
/*======================================================================*/
IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
	SET @Result = '1' /*OK*/
/**/
END










GO
-- [dbo].[CRMfnReturnCompanyNumber]



--ADBF890605
CREATE Function CRMfnReturnCompanyNumber()
Returns INT		-- Select dbo.CrmfnReturnCompanyNumber()
As
BEGIN

DECLARE @Number INT

SELECT @Number = ISNULL(MAX( cmpNumber), 0)
FROM dbo.CrmCompany

SET @Number = @Number + 1

RETURN(@Number)
end

-- [dbo].[CRMfnReturnProjectNumR]






/*RZ14000114*/
/*RZ14001209- Add ActivePrjNumRWithTitle*/
Create Function [dbo].[CRMfnReturnProjectNumR]
(	
	/* 
		Select dbo.CRMfnReturnProjectNumR(7651,N'���� �� �������')		
	*/
@CompanyId INT,
@prjTitle NVARCHAR(100)/*RZ14001209- Add ActivePrjNumRWithTitle*/
)
RETURNS BIGINT	
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @NumR BIGINT
	/**/
	/*-----------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14001209- Add ActivePrjNumRWithTitle*/
	DECLARE @ActivePrjNumRWithTitle BIT
	SET @ActivePrjNumRWithTitle = (SELECT TOP (1) [ActivePrjNumRWithTitle]
								   FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitles]
								   WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([ProjectTitle] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
										  = 
										 (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
										  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))))
	
	/*RZ14001209- End*/
	
	/*-----------------------------------*/
	IF(@CompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*SELECT @NumR = ISNULL(MAX([prjNumR]), 0)
		FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ*/
		/*RZ14001209- Add ActivePrjNumRWithTitle*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ActivePrjNumRWithTitle,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT @NumR = ISNULL(MAX([prjNumR]), 0)
			FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ActivePrjNumRWithTitle,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT @NumR = ISNULL(MAX([prjNumR]), 0)
			FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ
			WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([prjTitle] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
					= 
					(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14001209- End*/
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@CompanyId > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*SELECT @NumR = ISNULL(MAX([prjNumR]), 0)
		FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ
		WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId*/
		/*RZ14001209- Add ActivePrjNumRWithTitle*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ActivePrjNumRWithTitle,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT @NumR = ISNULL(MAX([prjNumR]), 0)
			FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ
			WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ActivePrjNumRWithTitle,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT @NumR = ISNULL(MAX([prjNumR]), 0)
			FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] PRJ
			WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId
			AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([prjTitle] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
					= 
					(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14001209- End*/
	/**/
	END
	
	/**/
	SET @NumR = @NumR + 1
	/**/
	RETURN(@NumR)
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMfnReturnValueInMultiLanguage]


/*RZ960218- use for multi laguage*/
Create Function [dbo].[CRMfnReturnValueInMultiLanguage]
(			
/*
	Select dbo.CRMfnReturnValueInMultiLanguage(1031, '@LangID=1033,%,test1033|@LangID=1031,%,test1031|')
 */
@LanguageID INT,
@Value NVARCHAR(MAX) 
)
Returns NVARCHAR(MAX) 		
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/

/*
input string => '@LangID=1033,%,test1033|@LangID=@LanguageID,%,test@LanguageID|'
change input string to list and
split langId and value
then langId check with @LanguageID
if condition is true 
return value 
*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------- BEGIN fill variable from parameters ------------------------------------*/	
	DECLARE @individual NVARCHAR(MAX) = null
	DECLARE @ReturnValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = null
	WHILE LEN(@Value) > 0
	BEGIN
		IF PATINDEX('%|%', @Value) > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @individual = SUBSTRING(@Value,
										0,
										PATINDEX('%|%', @Value))
			
			/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @paramValue NVARCHAR(4000)
			DECLARE @LangId VARCHAR(20)
			SET @LangId = SUBSTRING(@individual, CHARINDEX('@LangID=', @individual)+8, CHARINDEX(',%,',@individual) - (CHARINDEX('@LangID=', @individual)+8))                                   
			SET @paramValue = SUBSTRING(@individual, CHARINDEX(',%,', @individual)+3, Len(@individual))
			/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
			/**/
			IF(@LangId = CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(20))) 
      			SET @ReturnValue = @paramValue
		   /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
			SET @Value = SUBSTRING(@Value,
									  LEN(@individual + '|') + 1,
									  LEN(@Value))
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @individual = @Value
			SET @Value = NULL
		END
	END

	RETURN(@ReturnValue)
/**/
END


GO
-- [dbo].[CRMspAutoSendSMSAfterSaveMobile]







/*ADBF971012*/
/*RZ14000623- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspAutoSendSMSAfterSaveMobile]
(	/* 
		exec CRMspAutoSendSMSAfterSaveMobile  7387, 19326, '09396227682',3,'dasti',1065,0	
	*/
@CompanyId INT,
@CompanyPersonId INT,
@MobileNumber varchar(50),
@UserId int,
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
@SendNeedCheck INT
)
As
BEGIN
/* set @CompanyId = 19359	 set @CompanyPersonId = 34297  set @MobileNumber = '09396227682'  */
/**/
/**/
DECLARE 
  @MiladiDateFromsql datetime
, @PersianDateFromsql varchar(10)
, @calcSendDay NVARCHAR(50)
, @calcSendDate VARCHAR(8)
, @calcSendTime NVARCHAR(50)
, @calcSendDateTime DATETIME
, @Year1 INT
, @Month1 INT
, @RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8)
/**/
Set @MiladiDateFromsql = getdate()
Set @PersianDateFromsql = ( Select dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDate](CAST(@MiladiDateFromsql AS DATE)) )
Set @calcSendDay = (SELECT DATEPART(dw,@MiladiDateFromsql) ) 
Set @calcSendDate = ( SELECT SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 1, 4) + SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 6, 2) + SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 9, 2) )
Set @calcSendTime = (SELECT CONVERT(CHAR(8), @MiladiDateFromsql, 108))
Set @calcSendDateTime = @MiladiDateFromsql
Set @Year1 = ( SELECT SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 1, 4) ) 
Set @Month1 = ( SELECT SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 6, 2) )
Set @RegisterDatePersian = ( SELECT SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 1, 4) + SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 6, 2) + SUBSTRING(@PersianDateFromsql, 9, 2) )
/**/
DECLARE 
  @ActiveSendAfterRegNewNum bit, @SendAfterRegNewNumText nvarchar(1000), @SendAfterRegNewNumWeb bit, @SendAfterRegNewNumGSM bit
SELECT 
  @ActiveSendAfterRegNewNum = ActiveSendAfterRegNewNum, @SendAfterRegNewNumText = SendAfterRegNewNumText
, @SendAfterRegNewNumWeb = SendAfterRegNewNumWeb, @SendAfterRegNewNumGSM = SendAfterRegNewNumGSM 
From SmsSetting
/**/
/**/
Declare 
@PersonMobileNumber VARCHAR(50), @SendType INT, @SendTopic NVARCHAR(50), @SID INT, @smsBody NVARCHAR(2000), @SmsBodyLen INT, @SmsBodyLangType VARCHAR(50),
@SmsBodyPageCnt VARCHAR(50), @FromNumber VARCHAR(50), @SmsEnCost NUMERIC, @SmsFaCost NUMERIC, @SmsBodyCost NUMERIC, @NumberCount INT, @Numbers VARCHAR(max),
@RefNumbers VARCHAR(max), @AllSmsCount INT, @UserCost NUMERIC, @SendUserId INT, @SendDay NVARCHAR(50), @SendDate VARCHAR(8), @SendTime NVARCHAR(50), @SendDateTime DATETIME,
@CompaniesId VARCHAR(max), @CompanyPersonsId VARCHAR(max), /*@UserId INT, @Year1 INT, @Month1 INT, @RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),*/ @SingleNumber VARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@SingleCompanyId INT = NULL, @SingleCompanyPersonId INT = NULL, @SingleRefNumber BIGINT = NULL , /*@AppType NVARCHAR(50),*/ @StatusId INT, @CMessage INT, @SchedulDate DATETIME  = NULL,
@SendPriority INT = NULL /*@DefaultLanguageID INT*/
/**/
/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
if (@ActiveSendAfterRegNewNum = 1) and (@CompanyId > 0) and (@CompanyPersonId > 0) 
BEGIN
	DECLARE 
	  @ValueForCmpTitle NVARCHAR(1000) 
	, @ValueForCmpName NVARCHAR(1000) 
	, @ValueForPrsTitle NVARCHAR(1000) 
	, @ValueForPrsFName NVARCHAR(1000) 
	, @ValueForPrsLName NVARCHAR(1000) 
	SELECT
	@ValueForPrsTitle = (SELECT PersonTitle FROM CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleID = prsTitleId ),
	@ValueForPrsFName  = prsFName, 
	@ValueForPrsLName  = prsLName 
	FROM CrmCompanyPersons 
	WHERE CompanyPersonID = @CompanyPersonId

	

	/*SELECT 
		 @ValueForCmpTitle = (SELECT CompanyTitle FROM CrmCompanyTitle where CompanyTitleID =  cmpTitleId)
		, @ValueForCmpName = cmpName  
	FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE CompanyID = CompanyId*/	
	/*RZ14000623- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	SELECT 		
		@ValueForCmpTitle = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END),
			@ValueForCmpName =  (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)	
	/*FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE CompanyID = CompanyId*/
	FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId /*RZ14000623- Modify this sp*/
	/*RZ14000623- End*/

	
	/**/
	SET @SendAfterRegNewNumText = REPLACE(@SendAfterRegNewNumText, N'[#����� �����#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpTitle, '') )
	SET @SendAfterRegNewNumText = REPLACE(@SendAfterRegNewNumText, N'[#��� �����#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpName, '') )
	SET @SendAfterRegNewNumText = REPLACE(@SendAfterRegNewNumText, N'[#����� �����#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrsTitle, '') )
	SET @SendAfterRegNewNumText = REPLACE(@SendAfterRegNewNumText, N'[#��� �捘 �����#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrsFName, '') )
	SET @SendAfterRegNewNumText = REPLACE(@SendAfterRegNewNumText, N'[#��� �����ϐ� �����#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrsLName, '') )
	/**/
	/* print @SendAfterKnownNumCallText */
	/**/
	SET @PersonMobileNumber = @MobileNumber 
	SET @SendTopic = NULL
	SET @SID = (Select dbo.SmsfnReturnSID() )
	SET @smsBody = @SendAfterRegNewNumText
	SELECT 
		@SmsBodyLen = BodyLen, 	@SmsBodyLangType = BodyLang, @SmsBodyPageCnt = BodyPageCount
	FROM SmsReturnBodyTextLang(@smsBody)
	/**/
	SELECT TOP 1 
		@FromNumber = Number, 	@SmsEnCost = EnCost,  @SmsFaCost = FaCost FROM dbo.SmsSetting 
	/**/
	IF @SmsBodyLangType = 'En'
	SET @SmsBodyCost = ISNULL(@SmsEnCost, 0) * @SmsBodyPageCnt
	ELSE 
	IF @SmsBodyLangType = 'Fa'
	SET @SmsBodyCost = ISNULL(@SmsFaCost, 0) * @SmsBodyPageCnt
	/**/
	SET @NumberCount = 1   	SET @Numbers = @PersonMobileNumber   	SET @RefNumbers = '0,'  
	SET @AllSmsCount = @NumberCount * @SmsBodyPageCnt   SET @UserCost = @NumberCount * @SmsBodyPageCnt * @SmsBodyCost
	SET @SendUserId = @UserID   SET @SendDay = @calcSendDay   SET @SendDate = @calcSendDate   	SET @SendTime = @calcSendTime
	SET @SendDateTime = @calcSendDateTime    SET @CompaniesId = @CompanyId   SET @CompanyPersonsId = @CompanyPersonId
	SET @SingleNumber = @Numbers   	SET @SingleCompanyId = @CompanyId   	SET @SingleCompanyPersonId = @CompanyPersonId
	SET @SingleRefNumber = 0   	SET @StatusId = -2000   SET @CMessage = 1   SET @SchedulDate = NULL    	SET @SendPriority = 1
	/**/
	if (@SendAfterRegNewNumWeb = 1 ) AND (@PersonMobileNumber <> '') AND (@smsBody <> '')
	begin
	SET @SendType = 1
	EXEC dbo.SmsspInsertSendedSms 
		@SendType, @SendTopic, @SID, @FromNumber, @smsBody, @SmsBodyPageCnt, @SmsBodyLangType, @SmsBodyCost, @NumberCount,
		@Numbers, @RefNumbers, @AllSmsCount, @UserCost, @SendUserId, @SendDay, @SendDate, @SendTime, @SendDateTime, @CompaniesId,
		@CompanyPersonsId, @UserId, @Year1, @Month1, @RegisterDatePersian, @SingleNumber, @SingleCompanyId, @SingleCompanyPersonId,
		@SingleRefNumber, @AppType, @StatusId, @CMessage, @SchedulDate, @SendPriority, @DefaultLanguageID, @SendNeedCheck
	end
	/**/
	if (@SendAfterRegNewNumGSM = 1 ) AND (@PersonMobileNumber <> '') AND (@smsBody <> '')
	begin
	SET @SendType = 21
	EXEC dbo.SmsspInsertSendedSms 
		@SendType, @SendTopic, @SID, @FromNumber, @smsBody, @SmsBodyPageCnt, @SmsBodyLangType, @SmsBodyCost, @NumberCount, @Numbers, @RefNumbers,
		@AllSmsCount, @UserCost, @SendUserId, @SendDay, @SendDate, @SendTime, @SendDateTime, @CompaniesId, @CompanyPersonsId, @UserId, @Year1,
		@Month1, @RegisterDatePersian, @SingleNumber, @SingleCompanyId, @SingleCompanyPersonId, @SingleRefNumber, @AppType, @StatusId, @CMessage,
		@SchedulDate, @SendPriority, @DefaultLanguageID, @SendNeedCheck
	end

END

-- [dbo].[CRMspCheckActionForUser]






/*RZ14020604*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspCheckActionForUser]
/*
	DECLARE @HasAction BIT
	EXEC CRMspCheckActionForUser N'frmPreOrderDataEntry', N'actAutoCalcNumRef', 3, @HasAction OUTPUT
	SELECT @HasAction
*/
(
@FormName NVARCHAR(50),
@ActionName NVARCHAR(50),
@UserId INT,
@HasAction BIT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
		SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
		WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = @FormName )
		AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = @ActionName
							AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = @FormName ))
		AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		SET @HasAction = 1
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @HasAction = 0
	END
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspCreateCompanyUsernameAndPassword]






/*RZ14000420*/
/*RZ14000505- Chcke set password and username*/
/*RZ14001120-Change first person to cmpMainPerson*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspCreateCompanyUsernameAndPassword]
(
/* 
	exec CRMspCreateCompanyUsernameAndPassword 	6983,3,'2021-07-11','14000420','dasti'
*/
@CompanyId INT,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*==========================================================================*/
/*Set type from foSetting*/
DECLARE @CmpDefUsernameType INT,@CmpDefPasswordType INT
SELECT TOP 1
     @CmpDefUsernameType = [CmpDefUsernameType]
    ,@CmpDefPasswordType = [CmpDefPasswordType]
FROM [dbo].[CrmFoSetting]
/*Type = 0 => cmpNumber, Type = 1 => cmpMobile, Type = 2 => cmpEmail, Type=3 => prsNationalCode*/
/*==========================================================================*/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
BEGIN TRY	
	/*======================================================================*/
	DECLARE @CmpUserName NVARCHAR(50) = '',@CmpPassword NVARCHAR(50) = '',
	@NewUserName NVARCHAR(50) = '',@NewPassword NVARCHAR(50) = ''
	/*====================== Set UserName,Password ===============================*/		
	/*============== Set username ================*/
	IF(@CmpDefUsernameType = 0)/*cmpNumber*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewUserName = CAST(ISNULL(cmpNumber,0) AS NVARCHAR(50))	FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	ELSE IF(@CmpDefUsernameType = 1)/*cmpMobile*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewUserName = cmpMobile	FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	ELSE IF(@CmpDefUsernameType = 2)/*cmpEmail*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewUserName = cmpEmail	FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	ELSE IF(@CmpDefUsernameType = 3)/*prsNationalCode*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewUserName = (SELECT TOP 1 LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(prsNationalCode, '')))
								FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP 
								/*WHERE CP.prsCompanyId = CC.CompanyId AND ISNULL(CP.[prsWorkingOut], 0)  = 0
								ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID)*/
								/*RZ14001120-Change first person to cmpMainPerson*/
								WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = CC.cmpMainPersonId
								/*RZ14001120-End*/
								)	
		FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END			
	/**/
	IF((@CmpDefUsernameType IS NULL) AND EXISTS(SELECT cmpUserName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId AND ISNULL(cmpUserName,'') <> ''))/*When the company already has a username*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewUserName = cmpUserName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	/*============== Set password ================*/
	IF(@CmpDefPasswordType = 0)/*cmpNumber*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewPassword = CAST(ISNULL(cmpNumber,0) AS NVARCHAR(50))	FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	ELSE IF(@CmpDefPasswordType = 1)/*cmpMobile*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewPassword = cmpMobile	FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	ELSE IF(@CmpDefPasswordType = 2)/*cmpEmail*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewPassword = cmpEmail	FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	ELSE IF(@CmpDefPasswordType = 3)/*prsNationalCode*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewPassword = (SELECT TOP 1 LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(prsNationalCode, '')))
								FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP 
								/*WHERE CP.prsCompanyId = CC.CompanyId AND ISNULL(CP.[prsWorkingOut], 0)  = 0
								ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID)*/
								/*RZ14001120-Change first person to cmpMainPerson*/
								WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = CC.cmpMainPersonId
								/*RZ14001120-End*/
								)	
		FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END	
	ELSE IF(@CmpDefPasswordType = 4)/*random*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT @NewPassword = COALESCE(@NewPassword, '') + n /*Create a string random with 5 character*/
		FROM (SELECT TOP 5 CHAR(number) n FROM
		MASTER..spt_values
		WHERE TYPE = 'P' AND 
		(number BETWEEN ASCII(0) AND Ascii(9)
		OR number BETWEEN ASCII('A') AND ASCII('Z')
		OR number BETWEEN ASCII('a') AND ASCII('z'))
		ORDER BY NEWID()) a
		/**/
		
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	DECLARE @PasswordParII NVARCHAR(4) = '',@Second NVARCHAR(2) = '',@MilliSecond NVARCHAR(3) = ''
	SET @Second = DATEPART(SECOND,GETDATE())
	SET @MilliSecond = DATEPART(ms,GETDATE())
	IF(LEN(@Second) = 1) SET @Second = '0' + @Second
	IF(LEN(@MilliSecond) = 1) SET @MilliSecond = '00' + @MilliSecond
	IF(LEN(@MilliSecond) = 2) SET @MilliSecond = '0' + @MilliSecond
	SET @PasswordParII = CAST((LEFT(SUBSTRING(RTRIM(RAND()) + SUBSTRING(RTRIM(RAND()),3,11), 3,11),4)) AS NVARCHAR(4))		
	/*SET @NewPassword = @MilliSecond + @Second + @NewPassword + @PasswordParII*/					
	IF(ISNULL(@NewPassword,'') <> '' )
	BEGIN 
		SET @NewPassword = @MilliSecond + @Second + @NewPassword + @PasswordParII
	END
	/**/
	IF((@CmpDefPasswordType IS NULL) AND EXISTS(SELECT cmpPassword FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId AND ISNULL(cmpPassword,'') <> ''))/*When the company already has a password*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT @NewPassword = cmpPassword FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	END
	/*============ Update Company ===============*/
	/*UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
		SET cmpUserName = @NewUserName, 
			cmpPassword = @NewPassword,
			UserId = @UserId,
			Month1 = @Month1,
			Year1 = @Year1,
			RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
			RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			AppType =@AppType  
	WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId*/
	/*RZ14000505- Chcke set password and username*/
	IF(ISNULL(@NewUserName,'') <> '' AND ISNULL(@NewPassword,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
			SET cmpUserName = @NewUserName, 
				cmpPassword = @NewPassword,
				UserId = @UserId,
				Month1 = @Month1,
				Year1 = @Year1,
				RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
				RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
				AppType =@AppType  
		WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId	
		/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1 AS Result
	/**/
	END
	ELSE 
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT -1 AS Result
	/**/
	END		
	/*RZ14000505- End*/
	/**/
	/*COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1 AS Result*/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/*====================================================================================*/
/**/
END

Create procedure [dbo].[CRMspDeleteCompanyBackDependencies]
(
@CompanyId int
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
Delete SmsSendDetail
where CompanyId = @CompanyId

Delete SmsReceive
where CompanyId = @CompanyId

Delete SmsTempMobileNumbers
where CompanyId = @CompanyId

Delete MobCompanyAppInfo
where CompanyId = @CompanyId

Delete EmailUserTempTank
where EmailCmpId = @CompanyId

Delete EmailSendDetail
where EmailCmpId = @CompanyId

Delete CrmTelegContacts
where CompanyId = @CompanyId

Delete CrmTelegBotContacts
where CompanyId = @CompanyId

Delete CrmCallHistory
where CompanyId = @CompanyId

/*RZ971029- delete tmp*/
DELETE FROM [SmsTempMobileNumbers]
WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId

DELETE FROM [EmailUserTempTank]
WHERE [EmailCmpId] = @CompanyId
/*RZ971029- end*/
/*RZ14011123- Add CrmCompanyChangeHistory*/
DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory_Lang] 
WHERE [CompanyChangeHistoryId] IN(SELECT CCH.[CompanyChangeHistoryId] 
								  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory] CCH
								  WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId)
DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory] 
WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId
/*RZ14011123- End*/
/**/
END

(

	exec CRMspDeleteUserMoneyBagBySysPartAndDisSt 20,4752,8
*/
@UMnBgSystemPartId INT,
@UMnBgSystemPartObjectId INT,
@UMnBgDiscountStrategyId INT
)

BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		DECLARE @UserMoneyBagId INT = NULL,@UMnBgUserId INT = NULL	
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@UMnBgSystemPartId,0) = 20)/*Pord*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @UMnBgUserId = (SELECT TOP(1) pordReferUserId FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE PreOrderID = @UMnBgSystemPartObjectId )
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@UMnBgSystemPartId,0) = 30)/*Ord*/

		/**/
			SET @UMnBgUserId = (SELECT TOP(1) ordReferUserId FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE OrderID = @UMnBgSystemPartObjectId )
		/**/
		END
		/**/

		/*SET @UserMoneyBagId = (SELECT TOP (1) [UserMoneyBagId]      
								FROM [dbo].[UserMoneyBag]
								WHERE [UMnBgSystemPartId] = @UMnBgSystemPartId
								AND [UMnBgSystemPartObjectId] = @UMnBgSystemPartObjectId

																	FROM [dbo].[UserMoneyBag]

																AND [UMnBgDiscountStrategyId] = @UMnBgDiscountStrategyId

-- [dbo].[CRMspDyFormCheck]







/*RZ970824*/
/*RZ980329- Add FormId and Update masterValueDetail*/
/*RZ970404- Add Mode And remove FormId*/
/*RZ14010331- Check update CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails in for form has a workflow*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[CRMspDyFormCheck]				
(
/* 
	exec CRMspDyFormCheck 357, 3,'13970824' ,2
*/
@MasterValueId INT,
@CheckerUserId INT,
@CheckedDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
/*@FormId INT*//*RZ980329- Add FormId*/
/*RZ970404- Add Mode And remove FormId*/
@Mode INT /*mode=1 ===> form is normal, mode=2 ===> form is a workflow*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*for normal form*/
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 1
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
			  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
		WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
		/**/
		/*RZ980329- Update masterValueDetail*/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 1
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
			  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
		WHERE [MasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
		/*RZ980329- END*/
	END
	ELSE /*for form has a workflow*/
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
		   SET [IsChecked] = 1
			  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
			  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
			  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
			  /*RZ970404- Remove FormId*/
			  /*,[WorkflowStepId] = (SELECT TOP 1 WD.[FormWorkflowStepId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormWorkflowSteps] WD WHERE WD.[SubFormId] = @FormId)*/
		WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId	
		/*RZ14010331- Check update CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails in for form has a workflow*/
		DECLARE @FormId INT
		SET  @FormId = (SELECT TOP 1 [FormId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @MasterValueId) 
		IF(ISNULL(@FormId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
			   SET [IsChecked] = 1
				  ,[CheckerUserId] = @CheckerUserId
				  ,[CheckedDate] = GETDATE()
				  ,[CheckedDatePersian] = @CheckedDatePersian
			WHERE [MasterValueId] = @MasterValueId
			AND [FormId] = @FormId
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14010331- End*/
	END			
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspInsertCompanyChangeHistory]







/*RZ14011124*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspInsertCompanyChangeHistory]
/*
	declare @InsertedId int = 0
	exec CRMspInsertCompanyChangeHistory 
	@CompanyId = 68697,
	@FieldName = N'cmpReferUserId',
	@OldValue = N'56',
	@NewValue = N'3',
	@Desc = N'', 
	@CmpChangeHistory_Lang = NULL, 	
	@UserId = 3,
	@RegisterDate = '2023-02-13',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14011124',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,		
	@InsertedId = @InsertedId OUTPUT
	select @InsertedId 
*/
(
@CompanyId INT,
@FieldName NVARCHAR(300),
@OldValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@NewValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@Desc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, 
@CmpChangeHistory_Lang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, 
/**/
@UserId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
/**/
@InsertedId INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	/*============================================================================*/
	/*================================ Insert ====================================*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory]
        ([CompanyID]
        ,[FieldName]
        ,[OldValue]
        ,[NewValue]
        ,[Desc]
        ,[UserId]
        ,[RegisterDate]
        ,[RegisterDatePersian]        
        ,[AppType]
        ,[LanguageID])
    VALUES
        (@CompanyID
        ,@FieldName
        ,@OldValue
        ,@NewValue
        ,@Desc
        ,@UserId
        ,@RegisterDate
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,@AppType
        ,@DefaultLanguageID)
	SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()	
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@CmpChangeHistory_Lang,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory_Lang]
			([CompanyChangeHistoryId]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[OldValue]
			,[NewValue]
			,[Desc])
		SELECT 
			@InsertedId,
			JS.[LanguageID],
			JS.[OldValue],
			JS.[NewValue],
			JS.[Desc]
		FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@CmpChangeHistory_Lang,'[]'))
		WITH (
			[LanguageID] INT '$.LanguageID',
			[OldValue] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.OldValue',
			[NewValue] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.NewValue',
			[Desc] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Desc'
		) AS JS
		WHERE JS.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND JS.[LanguageID] IN(SELECT L.[LanguageID]
							   FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
							   WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1)
	/**/
	END		
	/*===========================================================================*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspInsertProjectInformationsDefualtField]



/*ADBF*/
/*ADBF9511 Add New Field For Multi Lang*/
create Procedure [dbo].[CRMspInsertProjectInformationsDefualtField]
(		/*-- exec CRMspInsertProjectInformationsDefualtField */
@ProjectID INT,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 INT,
@Month1 INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
/*ADBF9511*/
@LanguageID INT,
@FirstRegisterUserId INT,
@FirstRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(10),
@FirstRegisterDate DATETIME 
)
As
BEGIN
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmProjectInformations (
	ProjectId,
	FieldTitleId,
	/*--FieldValue,
	--Description,*/
	UserId,
	Year1,
	Month1,
	RegisterDate,
	RegisterDatePersian,
	AppType, 
	/*ADBF9511*/
	LanguageID,
	FirstRegisterUserId,
	FirstRegisterDatePersian,
	FirstRegisterDate
) 
SELECT 
	@ProjectID,
	ProjectFieldTitleId,
	@UserId,
	@Year1,
	@Month1,
	GETDATE(),
	@RegisterDatePersian,
	@AppType, 
	/*ADBF9511*/
	@LanguageID,
	@FirstRegisterUserId,
	@FirstRegisterDatePersian,
	@FirstRegisterDate 
FROM dbo.CrmProjectFieldTitle
WHERE IsDefualtField = 1
AND ProjectFieldTitleId NOT IN ( SELECT ProjectFieldTitleId 
				FROM CrmProjectFieldTitle
				WHERE ProjectId = @ProjectID)
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspInternalMessageSendSingle]






/*--911194119502ESP951019951102951103*/
/*ESP951211 Insert Language and first*/
/*RZ14000519- Add msgBody length*/
/*RZ14001207- Add fields*/
/*RZ14010928- Remove Type 12 in internal message and add wsDoc*/
/*AI14011216 - Add parameter*/
/*AI14020421 - Insert to UserPushNotifTank*/
/*RZ14020912- Add WSStockSupply*/
/*RZ14021005- Change IDENTITY*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspInternalMessageSendSingle]
(		
/*exec CRMspInternalMessageSendSingle*/
@msgMasterId INT = NULL,
@msgSubject NVARCHAR(200),
@msgBody NVARCHAR(MAX),/*RZ14000519- Add msgBody length- 2000 to max*/
/*--------------*/
@msgdTypeId INT ,
@msgdFromUserId INT ,
@msgdToUserId INT ,
@msgdSendDate VARCHAR(8),
@msgdSendTime VARCHAR(20),
@msgdSendDateTime DATETIME,
@msgdPriorityId INT,
/*--@msgdIsDraft BIT = NULL,
--@msgdIsDeleted BIT = NULL,*/
@msgdAttachFile NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@msgdAttachRecId INT = NULL,
@msgdAttachRecDesc NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@msgdGroupId INT = NULL,
/*---------------*/
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50)
,@InsertedID INT OUTPUT /*ESP951102*/
,@LanguageID INT = NULL /*ESP951103*/
/*AI14011216*/
,@msgdAttachRecIdI INT = NULL
,@msgdAttachRecDescI NVARCHAR(100) = NULL
/*AI14011216-End*/
)
As
BEGIN
/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------	
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--********************MAINTRAN*/
/*------------- Set CompanyId And ProjectId -----------------*/
/*RZ14001207- Add fields*/
DECLARE @msgCompanyId INT,
@msgProjectId INT
IF(ISNULL(@msgdAttachRecId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) IN(5,60)) /*PORD*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = pordCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = pordProjectId
		FROM WSPreOrder
		WHERE PreOrderID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 8) /*ORD*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = ordCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = ordProjectId
		FROM WSOrder
		WHERE OrderID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 9) /*BORD*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = bordCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = bordProjectId
		FROM WSBuyOrder
		WHERE BuyOrderID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) in(10,45,50)) /*CNVS*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = cnvsCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = cnvsProjectId
		FROM CrmConversation
		WHERE ConversationID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 14) /*LETT*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = LetterCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = LettercmpProjectId
		FROM SecLetters
		WHERE LetterId = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) IN(16,17,55)) /*DyForm*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = CompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = ProjectId
		FROM CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues
		WHERE FormFieldMasterValueId = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 18) /*CTSH*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyID FROM CrmCompany WHERE CompanyID = 
											(SELECT prjCompanyId FROM CrmProjects WHERE ProjectID = [ctshCmpProjectId])),
			@msgProjectId = [ctshCmpProjectId]
		FROM [WSCostSheets]
		WHERE [CostSheetId] = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14010928- Remove Type 12 in internal message and add wsDoc*/
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 20) /*DOC*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = CompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = ProjectId
		FROM [dbo].[WSDoc]
		WHERE DocID = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	/*RZ14010928- End*/
	/*RZ14020912- Add WSStockSupply*/
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@msgdTypeId,0) = 21) /*StockSupply*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT TOP 1
			@msgCompanyId = stsupCompanyId,
			@msgProjectId = stsupProjectId
		FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
		WHERE StockSupplyId = @msgdAttachRecId
	/**/
	END
	/*RZ14020912- End*/
/**/
END
/*RZ14001207- End*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------*/

	/**/
	DECLARE @upExtraData NVARCHAR(MAX) = 
				(SELECT @MessageID AS 'msgId', @msgdTypeId AS 'msgdTypeId', @msgdFromUserId AS 'msgdFromUserId' FOR JSON PATH)
	SET @upExtraData = REPLACE(@upExtraData, '[', '')
	SET @upExtraData = REPLACE(@upExtraData, ']', '')
	/**/
	EXEC [dbo].[CRMspInsertUserPushNotifTank]
		@upTypeId = 1
		,@upUserIds = @NotifUsers
		,@upTitle = @msgSubject
		,@upBody = NULL
		,@upExtraData = @upExtraData
		,@upPriority = NULL
		,@UserId = @UserId
		,@Year1 = @Year1
		,@Month1 = @Month1
		,@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate
		,@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
		,@AppType = @AppType
		,@LanguageID = @LanguageID
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
END CATCH
/*AI14020421-End*/

IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	/*--Set @OrderId = 0
	--Return @OrderId*/
	SET @InsertedID = -1  /*ESP951102*/
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	/*--Return @OrderId*/
	SET @InsertedID = @MessageID  /*ESP951102*/
END	
/*--****/	 
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspLeaveVerify]
/*
	
	EXEC CRMspLeaveVerify 867,3,'2022-12-17','14010926'
*/		
(
@LeaveId INT,
@VerifierUserId INT,
@VerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME,
@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8)
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmLeave]
	SET 
		[LeaveStatusId] = 2
	   ,[VerifierUserId] = @VerifierUserId
	   ,[VerifiedRegisterDate] = @VerifiedRegisterDate
	   ,[VerifiedRegisterDatePersian] = @VerifiedRegisterDatePersian
	WHERE [LeaveId] = @LeaveId
/**/
END

END

-- [dbo].[CRMspReturnProjectNumRef]






/*RZ991213*/
/*RZ14000114- Remove prjInquiryNumR and add prjNumR*/
/*RZ14000616- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspReturnProjectNumRef]
(	
/* 
	exec CRMspReturnProjectNumRef 6156,'ddrd',1452,3,1033,1065	
*/
@PrjCompanyId INT,
@PrjInquiryNum NVARCHAR(100),
/*@PrjInquiryNumR BIGINT,*/
@PrjNumR BIGINT,/*RZ14000114- Remove prjInquiryNumR and add prjNumR*/
@PrjStarterUserId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*------------------------ Define variabels -------------------*/
DECLARE 
@ValueForCmpName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpNameEng NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpNumber NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpCode NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPrjInquiryNum NVARCHAR(1000),
/*@ValueForPrjInquiryNumR NVARCHAR(1000),*/
@ValueForPrjNumR NVARCHAR(1000),/*RZ14000114- Remove prjInquiryNumR and add prjNumR*/
@ValueForSysNum NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForSysNumR NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserFName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserLName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserFullName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserPersCode NVARCHAR(1000),
@prjNumRef NVARCHAR(150),
@prjNumRefFormula NVARCHAR(4000)
/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP 1 
    @prjNumRefFormula = [prjNumRefFormula]    
FROM [dbo].[CrmNumRefSetting]
/*------------------ Check prjNumRefFormula -----------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@prjNumRefFormula,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*------------------------ Set variabels -------------------*/
	/*SELECT 
		@ValueForCmpName = cmpName, 
		@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
		@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
		@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
	FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
	WHERE CompanyID = @PrjCompanyId*/

	/*RZ14000616- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
									   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
											  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
									   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
													ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
											FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
											WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
											)				
								 END), 
			@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @PrjCompanyId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
									THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
											ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
									ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
												ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
										WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
										)				
								END), 
			@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @PrjCompanyId
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14000616- End*/



	SET @ValueForSysNum = @PrjInquiryNum
	/*SET @ValueForSysNumR =  CAST(@PrjInquiryNumR AS NVARCHAR(50))*/
	/*RZ14000114- Remove prjInquiryNumR and add prjNumR*/
	SET @ValueForSysNumR =  CAST(@PrjNumR AS NVARCHAR(50))

	SET @ValueForPrjInquiryNum = @PrjInquiryNum
	/*SET @ValueForPrjInquiryNumR =  CAST(@PrjInquiryNumR AS NVARCHAR(50))*/
	/*RZ14000114- Remove prjInquiryNumR and add prjNumR*/
	SET @ValueForPrjNumR =  CAST(@PrjNumR AS NVARCHAR(50))

	/*SELECT 
		@ValueForUserFName = [FName],
		@ValueForUserLName = [LName],
		@ValueForUserFullName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL([FName],'') + ' ' +  ISNULL([LName],''))),
		@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
	FROM [dbo].[Users] U
	WHERE U.[UserId] = @PrjStarterUserId*/


	/*RZ14000616- Add multiLang*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = [FName],
			@ValueForUserLName = [LName],
			@ValueForUserFullName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL([FName],'') + ' ' +  ISNULL([LName],''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @PrjStarterUserId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserLName = ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserFullName =  LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') 
								  + ' ' 
								  +  ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @PrjStarterUserId
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14000616- End*/

	/*------------------------ Set NumRef ---------------------*/
	SET @prjNumRef = @prjNumRefFormula
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpNameEng#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNameEng, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpNumber#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNumber, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpCode, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#SysNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNum, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#SysNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNumR, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#PrjInquiryNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjInquiryNum, '') )
	/*SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#PrjInquiryNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjInquiryNumR, '') )*/
	/*RZ14000114- Remove prjInquiryNumR and add prjNumR*/
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#PrjNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumR, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserFName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserLName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserLName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserFullName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFullName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserPersCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserPersCode, '') )	
/**/
END
/**/
SELECT @prjNumRef [prjNumRef]
/**/
END









GO
-- [dbo].[CRMspSetcmpDataPermission]

/*--ADBF9302039410*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspSetcmpDataPermission]
(		/*--	EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission 3 */
@UserId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(4000)
SET @Term = '';
/*--Province*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpProvinceId Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpProvinceId IN (
SELECT JustAccessProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--City*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpCityId Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.cmpCityId IN (
SELECT JustAccessCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--GroupIV*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) IN (
SELECT JustAccessGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--Company*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.CompanyId Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND C.CompanyId IN (
SELECT JustAccessCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--Group*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) IN (
SELECT JustAccessGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--SubGroup*/
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) Not IN (
SELECT ForbiddenSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
SET @Term = @Term +
' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) IN (
SELECT JustAccessSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
/*--*/
/*--cmpReferUser*/
SET @Term = @Term +
' And C.cmpReferUserId IN( 
(SELECT '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION 
SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ 
CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 14 ) ) '
/*
--
--
--
--
*/
DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
	UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
 VALUES ( @UserId, @Term, 20 ) 
END

-- [dbo].[fnGregorianToPersianDate]







/*RZ960502*/
/*Convert Gregorian to Persian */
/*RZ990128- Convert DateTiem to date*/
Create Function [dbo].[fnGregorianToPersianDate] (@date DATETIME)
/*
	Select dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDate]('2017-07-24') as [Persian Date]
*/
RETURNS NVARCHAR(50)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @date = CAST(@date AS DATE)/*RZ990128- Convert DateTiem to date*/
    DECLARE @depoch AS BIGINT
    DECLARE @cycle  AS BIGINT
    DECLARE @cyear  AS BIGINT
    DECLARE @ycycle AS BIGINT
    DECLARE @aux1 AS BIGINT
    DECLARE @aux2 AS BIGINT
    DECLARE @yday AS BIGINT
    DECLARE @Jofst  AS NUMERIC(18,2)
    DECLARE @jdn BIGINT
    DECLARE @iYear   AS INTEGER
    DECLARE @iMonth  AS INTEGER
    DECLARE @iDay    AS INTEGER

    SET @Jofst=2415020.5
    SET @jdn=Round(CAST(@date AS INT)+ @Jofst,0)

    SET @depoch = @jdn - [dbo].[fnPersianToJulianDate](475, 1, 1) 
    SET @cycle = CAST(@depoch / 1029983 AS INT) 
    SET @cyear = @depoch%1029983 

    IF @cyear = 1029982
       BEGIN
         SET @ycycle = 2820 
       END
    ELSE
       BEGIN
        SET @aux1 = CAST(@cyear / 366 AS INT) 
        SET @aux2 = @cyear%366 
        SET @ycycle = CAST(((2134 * @aux1) + (2816 * @aux2) + 2815) / 1028522 AS INT) + @aux1 + 1 
      END

    SET @iYear = @ycycle + (2820 * @cycle) + 474 

    IF @iYear <= 0
      BEGIN 
        SET @iYear = @iYear - 1 
      END
    Set @yday = (@jdn - dbo.fnPersianToJulianDate(@iYear, 1, 1)) + 1 
    IF @yday <= 186 
       BEGIN
         SET @iMonth = CEILING(CONVERT(NUMERIC(18,4),@yday) / 31) 
       END
    ELSE
       BEGIN
          SET @iMonth = CEILING((CONVERT(NUMERIC(18,4),@yday) - 6) / 30)  
       END
       SET @iDay = (@jdn - dbo.fnPersianToJulianDate(@iYear, @iMonth, 1)) + 1 

	DECLARE @DayStr NVARCHAR(50)
	DECLARE @MonthStr NVARCHAR(50)
	IF(@iDay<10)
		SET @DayStr = '0' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),@iDay)
	ELSE
		SET @DayStr = CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),@iDay)
		
	IF(@iMonth<10)
		SET @MonthStr = '0' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),@iMonth)
	ELSE
		SET @MonthStr = CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),@iMonth)
	
	
      RETURN CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),@iYear) + '/' + @MonthStr + '/' + @DayStr 
/**/
END





GO
-- [dbo].[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash]





/*RZ960502*/
/*Convert Gregorian to Persian */
/*RZ990128- Convert DateTiem to date*/
/*AI14010210 - bug: leap year not considered in this function (for example '2025-03-20'). using a new way. old sp moved to _old*/
/*AI14010510 - Change format*/
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash] (@date DATETIME)
/*
	Select dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash]('2025-03-20 00:00:00') as [Persian Date]
*/
RETURNS NVARCHAR(50)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	/*RETURN FORMAT(@date, 'yyyyMMdd', 'fa') - AI14010510*/
	RETURN FORMAT(@date, 'yyyyMMdd', 'fa-IR')
/**/
END







GO
