/*
	Select dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash]('2025-03-20 00:00:00') as [Persian Date]
*/
RETURNS NVARCHAR(50)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	RETURN FORMAT(@date, 'yyyy/MM/dd', 'fa-IR')
/**/
END

/*RZ980225*/
Create FUNCTION [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords] ( 
    @Number Numeric (38, 0) /* Input number with as many as 18 digits*/
/*
	Select dbo.[fnNumberToArWords] (947)
	Select dbo.fnNumberToArWords (7446744073709551616)	
*/ 
) RETURNS NVARCHAR(MAX) 
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @inputNumber NVARCHAR(38)
DECLARE @NumbersTable TABLE (number INT, word NVARCHAR(20))
DECLARE @outputString NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @length INT
DECLARE @counter INT
DECLARE @loops INT
DECLARE @position INT
DECLARE @chunk NCHAR(3)
DECLARE @tensones NCHAR(2)
DECLARE @hundreds NCHAR(1)
DECLARE @tens NCHAR(1)
DECLARE @ones NCHAR(1)
DECLARE @And NVARCHAR(3)
DECLARE @Neg NVARCHAR(10)
 
    IF @Number = 0 return N'���'
     
    IF  Left(@Number ,1) <> '-'
        SET  @Neg = N' '
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET  @Neg = N'���� '
        SET  @Number = @Number * -1
    END
 
SELECT @inputNumber = CONVERT(NVARCHAR(38), @Number)
     , @outputString = ''
     , @counter = 1
SELECT @length   = LEN(@inputNumber)
     , @position = LEN(@inputNumber) - 2
     , @loops    = LEN(@inputNumber)/3
 
 
IF LEN(@inputNumber) % 3 <> 0 SET @loops = @loops + 1
 
 
INSERT INTO @NumbersTable   SELECT 0, N''
    UNION ALL SELECT 1, N' ���� '      UNION ALL SELECT 2, N' ����� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 3, N' ����� '    UNION ALL SELECT 4, N' ����� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 5, N' ���� '     UNION ALL SELECT 6, N' ��� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 7, N' ���� '    UNION ALL SELECT 8, N' ������ '
    UNION ALL SELECT 9, N' ���� '     UNION ALL SELECT 10, N' ���� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 11, N' ��� ��� '   UNION ALL SELECT 12, N' ���� ��� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 13, N' ����� ��� ' UNION ALL SELECT 14, N' ����� ���� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 15, N' ���� ��� '  UNION ALL SELECT 16, N' ������ ��� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 17, N' ���� ��� ' UNION ALL SELECT 18, N' ������� ��� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 19, N' ���� ��� ' UNION ALL SELECT 20, N' ����� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 30, N' ������ '   UNION ALL SELECT 40, N' ������ '
    UNION ALL SELECT 50, N' ����� '    UNION ALL SELECT 60, N' ���� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 70, N' ����� '  UNION ALL SELECT 80, N' ������ '
    UNION ALL SELECT 90, N' ����� '   UNION ALL SELECT 100, N' ���� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 200, N' ������ '   UNION ALL SELECT 300, N' ���� ��� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 400, N' ����� ��� '   UNION ALL SELECT 500, N' ������� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 600, N' ������ '   UNION ALL SELECT 700, N' ������� '
    UNION ALL SELECT 800, N' ���� ���� '   UNION ALL SELECT 900, N' ������� '
 
 
WHILE @counter <= @loops BEGIN
 
    SET @chunk = RIGHT('000' + SUBSTRING(@inputNumber, @position, 3), 3)
     
 
    IF @chunk <> '000' BEGIN
        SELECT @tensones = SUBSTRING(@chunk, 2, 2)
             , @hundreds = SUBSTRING(@chunk, 1, 1)
             , @tens = SUBSTRING(@chunk, 2, 1)
             , @ones = SUBSTRING(@chunk, 3, 1)
 
        IF CONVERT(INT, @tensones) <= 20 OR @Ones='0' BEGIN
                if len(@outputString)>0
                    begin
                    set @And=N'� '
                    end
                else
                    begin
                    set @And=N''
                    end
                     
                SET @outputString = (SELECT word
                                    FROM @NumbersTable
                                    WHERE @hundreds+'00'  = number)
                            + case @hundreds when '0' then '' else 
                                case @tensones when '00' then N'' else N'�' end  end+
                            (
                                    SELECT word
                                      FROM @NumbersTable
                                      WHERE @tensones = number)
                   + CASE @counter WHEN 1 THEN N'' /* No name*/
                       WHEN 2 THEN N' ��� ' WHEN 3 THEN N' ����� '
                       WHEN 4 THEN N' ����� '  WHEN 5 THEN N' ����� '
                       WHEN 6 THEN N' ������� ' WHEN 7 THEN N' ���������� '
                       WHEN 8 THEN N' Ә������� '  WHEN 9 THEN N' �������� '
                       WHEN 10 THEN N' ǘ������ '  WHEN 11 THEN N' �������� '
                       WHEN 12 THEN N' ������� '  WHEN 13 THEN N' ��������� '
                       ELSE N'' END
                               + @And + @outputString
            END
 
         ELSE BEGIN
 
                    if len(@outputString)>0
                    begin
                    set @And=N'� '
                    end
                else
                    begin
                    set @And=N''
                    end
                     
                 
 
                SET @outputString = N' '
                            + (SELECT word
                                    FROM @NumbersTable
                                    WHERE @hundreds+'00'  = number)
                             + case @hundreds when '0' then N'' else  N'�' end
                            + (SELECT word
                                    FROM @NumbersTable
                                    WHERE   @tens+'0'  = number)
                             + N'�'
                             + (SELECT word
                                    FROM @NumbersTable
                                    WHERE  @ones = number)
                   + CASE @counter WHEN 1 THEN N'' /* No name*/
                       WHEN 2 THEN N' ��� ' WHEN 3 THEN N' ����� '
                       WHEN 4 THEN N' ����� '  WHEN 5 THEN N' ����� '
                       WHEN 6 THEN N' ������� ' WHEN 7 THEN N' ���������� '
                       WHEN 8 THEN N' Ә������� '  WHEN 9 THEN N' �������� '
                       WHEN 10 THEN N' ǘ������ '  WHEN 11 THEN N' �������� '
                       WHEN 12 THEN N' ������� '  WHEN 13 THEN N' ��������� '
                       ELSE N'' END
                            + @And + @outputString
        END
         
 
    END
 
    SELECT @counter = @counter + 1
         , @position = @position - 3
 
END
 
SET @outputString = LTRIM(RTRIM(REPLACE(@outputString, N'  ', N' ')))
SET @outputstring = UPPER(LEFT(@outputstring, 1)) + SUBSTRING(@outputstring, 2, 8000)
SET @outputstring = @Neg + @outputstring
 
RETURN @outputString

/**/
END

-- [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords]





/*RZ980225*/
Create FUNCTION [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](@Number AS BIGINT)
/*
	Select dbo.[fnNumberToEnWords] (947)
	Select dbo.fnNumberToEnWords (7446744073709551616)		
*/
RETURNS NVARCHAR(MAX)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
      DECLARE @Below20 TABLE (ID INT  IDENTITY(0,1), Word VARCHAR(32))

      DECLARE @Below100 TABLE (ID INT IDENTITY(2,1), Word VARCHAR(32))

      INSERT @Below20 (Word) VALUES

                        ( 'zero'), ('one'),( 'two' ), ( 'three'),

                        ( 'four' ), ( 'five' ), ( 'six' ), ( 'seven' ),

                        ( 'eight'), ( 'nine'), ( 'ten'), ( 'eleven' ),

                        ( 'twelve' ), ( 'thirteen' ), ( 'fourteen'),

                        ( 'fifteen' ), ('sixteen' ), ( 'seventeen'),

                        ('eighteen' ), ( 'nineteen' )

       INSERT @Below100 VALUES ('twenty'), ('thirty'),('forty'), ('fifty'),

                               ('sixty'), ('seventy'), ('eighty'), ('ninety')

DECLARE @English  NVARCHAR(MAX) =

(

  SELECT Case

    WHEN @Number = 0 THEN  ''

    WHEN @Number BETWEEN 1 AND 19

      THEN (SELECT Word FROM @Below20 WHERE ID=@Number)

   WHEN @Number BETWEEN 20 AND 99  

     THEN  (SELECT Word FROM @Below100 WHERE ID=@Number/10)+ '-' +

           dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 10)

   WHEN @Number BETWEEN 100 AND 999  

     THEN  (dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number / 100))+' hundred '+

         dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 100)

   WHEN @Number BETWEEN 1000 AND 999999  

     THEN  (dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number / 1000))+' thousand '+

         dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 1000) 

   WHEN @Number BETWEEN 1000000 AND 999999999  

     THEN  (dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number / 1000000))+' million '+

         dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 1000000)

   WHEN @Number BETWEEN 1000000000 AND 999999999999  

     THEN  (dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number / 1000000000))+' billion '+

         dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 1000000000)

   WHEN @Number BETWEEN 1000000000000 AND 999999999999999  

     THEN  (dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number / 1000000000000))+' trillion '+

         dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 1000000000000)

  WHEN @Number BETWEEN 1000000000000000 AND 999999999999999999  

     THEN  (dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number / 1000000000000000))+' quadrillion '+

         dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 1000000000000000)

  WHEN @Number BETWEEN 1000000000000000000 AND 999999999999999999999  

     THEN  (dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number / 1000000000000000000))+' quintillion '+

         dbo.fnNumberToEnWords( @Number % 1000000000000000000)

        ELSE ' INVALID INPUT' END

)

SELECT @English = RTRIM(@English)

SELECT @English = RTRIM(LEFT(@English,LEN(@English)-1))

                 WHERE RIGHT(@English,1)='-'

RETURN (@English)
/**/
END

GO
-- [dbo].[fnParseJSON]






/*RZ980416*/
/*RZ991111-Modify for negetive number*/
Create FUNCTION [dbo].[fnParseJSON]( @JSON NVARCHAR(MAX))
/*
	 Select * from fnParseJSON('{}')
*/
	RETURNS @hierarchy TABLE
	  (
	   Element_ID INT IDENTITY(1, 1) NOT NULL, /* internal surrogate primary key gives the order of parsing and the list order */
	   SequenceNo [int] NULL, /* the place in the sequence for the element */
	   Parent_ID INT null, /* if the element has a parent then it is in this column. The document is the ultimate parent, so you can get the structure from recursing from the document */
	   Object_ID INT null, /* each list or object has an object id. This ties all elements to a parent. Lists are treated as objects here */
	   Name NVARCHAR(2000) NULL, /* the Name of the object */
	   StringValue NVARCHAR(MAX) NOT NULL,/*the string representation of the value of the element. */
	   ValueType VARCHAR(10) NOT null /* the declared type of the value represented as a string in StringValue*/
	  )
	  /*
 
	   */
	AS
	BEGIN
	  DECLARE
	    @FirstObject INT, /*the index of the first open bracket found in the JSON string*/
	    @OpenDelimiter INT,/*the index of the next open bracket found in the JSON string*/
	    @NextOpenDelimiter INT,/*the index of subsequent open bracket found in the JSON string*/
	    @NextCloseDelimiter INT,/*the index of subsequent close bracket found in the JSON string*/
	    @Type NVARCHAR(10),/*whether it denotes an object or an array*/
	    @NextCloseDelimiterChar CHAR(1),/*either a '}' or a ']'*/
	    @Contents NVARCHAR(MAX), /*the unparsed contents of the bracketed expression*/
	    @Start INT, /*index of the start of the token that you are parsing*/
	    @end INT,/*index of the end of the token that you are parsing*/
	    @param INT,/*the parameter at the end of the next Object/Array token*/
	    @EndOfName INT,/*the index of the start of the parameter at end of Object/Array token*/
	    @token NVARCHAR(200),/*either a string or object*/
	    @value NVARCHAR(MAX), /* the value as a string*/
	    @SequenceNo int, /* the sequence number within a list*/
	    @Name NVARCHAR(200), /*the Name as a string*/
	    @Parent_ID INT,/*the next parent ID to allocate*/
	    @lenJSON INT,/*the current length of the JSON String*/
	    @characters NCHAR(36),/*used to convert hex to decimal*/
	    @result BIGINT,/*the value of the hex symbol being parsed*/
	    @index SMALLINT,/*used for parsing the hex value*/
	    @Escape INT /*the index of the next escape character*/
	    
	  DECLARE @Strings TABLE /* in this temporary table we keep all strings, even the Names of the elements, since they are 'escaped' in a different way, and may contain, unescaped, brackets denoting objects or lists. These are replaced in the JSON string by tokens representing the string */
	    (
	     String_ID INT IDENTITY(1, 1),
	     StringValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
	    )
	  SELECT/*initialise the characters to convert hex to ascii*/
	    @characters='0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz',
	    @SequenceNo=0, /*set the sequence no. to something sensible.*/
	  /* firstly we process all strings. This is done because [{} and ] aren't escaped in strings, which complicates an iterative parse. */
	    @Parent_ID=0;
	  WHILE 1=1 /*forever until there is nothing more to do*/
	    BEGIN
	      SELECT
	        @start=PATINDEX('%[^a-zA-Z]["]%', @json collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin);/*next delimited string*/
	      IF @start=0 BREAK /*no more so drop through the WHILE loop*/
	      IF SUBSTRING(@json, @start+1, 1)='"' 
	        BEGIN /*Delimited Name*/
	          SET @start=@Start+1;
	          SET @end=PATINDEX('%[^\]["]%', RIGHT(@json, LEN(@json+'|')-@start) collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin);
	        END
	      IF @end=0 /*either the end or no end delimiter to last string*/
	        BEGIN/* check if ending with a double slash...*/
             SET @end=PATINDEX('%[\][\]["]%', RIGHT(@json, LEN(@json+'|')-@start) collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin);
 		     IF @end=0 /*we really have reached the end*/ 
				BEGIN
				BREAK /*assume all tokens found*/
				END
			END 
	      SELECT @token=SUBSTRING(@json, @start+1, @end-1)
	      /*now put in the escaped control characters*/
	      SELECT @token=REPLACE(@token, FromString, ToString)
	      FROM
	        (SELECT           '\b', CHAR(08)
	         UNION ALL SELECT '\f', CHAR(12)
	         UNION ALL SELECT '\n', CHAR(10)
	         UNION ALL SELECT '\r', CHAR(13)
	         UNION ALL SELECT '\t', CHAR(09)
			 UNION ALL SELECT '\"', '"'
	         UNION ALL SELECT '\/', '/'
	        ) substitutions(FromString, ToString)
		SELECT @token=Replace(@token, '\\', '\')
	      SELECT @result=0, @escape=1
	  /*Begin to take out any hex escape codes*/
	      WHILE @escape>0
	        BEGIN
	          SELECT @index=0,
	          /*find the next hex escape sequence*/
	          @escape=PATINDEX('%\x[0-9a-f][0-9a-f][0-9a-f][0-9a-f]%', @token collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin)
	          IF @escape>0 /*if there is one*/
	            BEGIN
	              WHILE @index<4 /*there are always four digits to a \x sequence   */
	                BEGIN
	                  SELECT /*determine its value*/
	                    @result=@result+POWER(16, @index)
	                    *(CHARINDEX(SUBSTRING(@token, @escape+2+3-@index, 1),
	                                @characters)-1), @index=@index+1 ;
	         
	                END
	                /* and replace the hex sequence by its unicode value*/
	              SELECT @token=STUFF(@token, @escape, 6, NCHAR(@result))
	            END
	        END
	      /*now store the string away*/ 
	      INSERT INTO @Strings (StringValue) SELECT @token
	      /* and replace the string with a token*/
	      SELECT @JSON=STUFF(@json, @start, @end+1,
	                    '@string'+CONVERT(NCHAR(5), @@identity))
	    END
	  /* all strings are now removed. Now we find the first leaf.  */
	  WHILE 1=1  /*forever until there is nothing more to do*/
	  BEGIN
	 
	  SELECT @Parent_ID=@Parent_ID+1
	  /*find the first object or list by looking for the open bracket*/
	  SELECT @FirstObject=PATINDEX('%[{[[]%', @json collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin)/*object or array*/
	  IF @FirstObject = 0 BREAK
	  IF (SUBSTRING(@json, @FirstObject, 1)='{') 
	    SELECT @NextCloseDelimiterChar='}', @type='object'
	  ELSE 
	    SELECT @NextCloseDelimiterChar=']', @type='array'
	  SELECT @OpenDelimiter=@firstObject
	  WHILE 1=1 /*find the innermost object or list...*/
	    BEGIN
	      SELECT
	        @lenJSON=LEN(@JSON+'|')-1
	  /*find the matching close-delimiter proceeding after the open-delimiter*/
	      SELECT
	        @NextCloseDelimiter=CHARINDEX(@NextCloseDelimiterChar, @json,
	                                      @OpenDelimiter+1)
	  /*is there an intervening open-delimiter of either type*/
	      SELECT @NextOpenDelimiter=PATINDEX('%[{[[]%',
	             RIGHT(@json, @lenJSON-@OpenDelimiter)collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin)/*object*/
	      IF @NextOpenDelimiter=0 
	        BREAK
	      SELECT @NextOpenDelimiter=@NextOpenDelimiter+@OpenDelimiter
	      IF @NextCloseDelimiter<@NextOpenDelimiter 
	        BREAK
	      IF SUBSTRING(@json, @NextOpenDelimiter, 1)='{' 
	        SELECT @NextCloseDelimiterChar='}', @type='object'
	      ELSE 
	        SELECT @NextCloseDelimiterChar=']', @type='array'
	      SELECT @OpenDelimiter=@NextOpenDelimiter
	    END
	  /*and parse out the list or Name/value pairs*/
	  SELECT
	    @contents=SUBSTRING(@json, @OpenDelimiter+1,
	                        @NextCloseDelimiter-@OpenDelimiter-1)
	  SELECT
	    @JSON=STUFF(@json, @OpenDelimiter,
	                @NextCloseDelimiter-@OpenDelimiter+1,
	                '@'+@type+CONVERT(NCHAR(5), @Parent_ID))
	  WHILE (PATINDEX('%[A-Za-z0-9@+-.e]%', @contents collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin))<>0/*RZ991111-Modify for negetive number*/ 
	    BEGIN
	      IF @Type='object' /*it will be a 0-n list containing a string followed by a string, number,boolean, or null*/
	        BEGIN
	          SELECT
	            @SequenceNo=0,@end=CHARINDEX(':', ' '+@contents)/*if there is anything, it will be a string-based Name.*/
	          SELECT  @start=PATINDEX('%[^A-Za-z@][@]%', ' '+@contents collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin)/*AAAAAAAA*/
              SELECT @token=RTrim(Substring(' '+@contents, @start+1, @End-@Start-1)),
	            @endofName=PATINDEX('%[0-9]%', @token collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin),
	            @param=RIGHT(@token, LEN(@token)-@endofName+1)
	          SELECT
	            @token=LEFT(@token, @endofName-1),
	            @Contents=RIGHT(' '+@contents, LEN(' '+@contents+'|')-@end-1)
	          SELECT  @Name=StringValue FROM @strings
	            WHERE string_id=@param /*fetch the Name*/
	        END
	      ELSE 
	        SELECT @Name=null,@SequenceNo=@SequenceNo+1 
	      SELECT
	        @end=CHARINDEX(',', @contents)/* a string-token, object-token, list-token, number,boolean, or null*/
                IF @end=0
	        /*HR Engineering notation bugfix start*/
	          IF ISNUMERIC(@contents) = 1
		    SELECT @end = LEN(@contents) + 1
	          Else
	        /*HR Engineering notation bugfix end */
		  SELECT  @end=PATINDEX('%[A-Za-z0-9@+-.e][^A-Za-z0-9@+-.e]%', @contents+' ' collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin) + 1/*RZ991111-Modify for negetive number*/
	       SELECT
	        @start=PATINDEX('%[^A-Za-z0-9@+-.e][A-Za-z0-9@+-.e]%', ' '+@contents collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin)/*RZ991111-Modify for negetive number*/
	      /*select @start,@end, LEN(@contents+'|'), @contents */ 
	      SELECT
	        @Value=RTRIM(SUBSTRING(@contents, @start, @End-@Start)),
	        @Contents=RIGHT(@contents+' ', LEN(@contents+'|')-@end)
	      IF SUBSTRING(@value, 1, 7)='@object' 
	        INSERT INTO @hierarchy
	          (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, Object_ID, ValueType)
	          SELECT @Name, @SequenceNo, @Parent_ID, SUBSTRING(@value, 8, 5),
	            SUBSTRING(@value, 8, 5), 'object' 
	      ELSE 
	        IF SUBSTRING(@value, 1, 6)='@array' 
	          INSERT INTO @hierarchy
	            (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, Object_ID, ValueType)
	            SELECT @Name, @SequenceNo, @Parent_ID, SUBSTRING(@value, 7, 5),
	              SUBSTRING(@value, 7, 5), 'array' 
	        ELSE 
	          IF SUBSTRING(@value, 1, 7)='@string' 
	            INSERT INTO @hierarchy
	              (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, ValueType)
	              SELECT @Name, @SequenceNo, @Parent_ID, StringValue, 'string'
	              FROM @strings
	              WHERE string_id=SUBSTRING(@value, 8, 5)
	          ELSE 
	            IF @value IN ('true', 'false') 
	              INSERT INTO @hierarchy
	                (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, ValueType)
	                SELECT @Name, @SequenceNo, @Parent_ID, @value, 'boolean'
	            ELSE
	              IF @value='null' 
	                INSERT INTO @hierarchy
	                  (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, ValueType)
	                  SELECT @Name, @SequenceNo, @Parent_ID, @value, 'null'
	              ELSE
	                IF PATINDEX('%[^0-9]%', @value collate SQL_Latin1_General_CP850_Bin)>0 
	                  INSERT INTO @hierarchy
	                    (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, ValueType)
	                    SELECT @Name, @SequenceNo, @Parent_ID, @value, 'float'
	                ELSE
	                  INSERT INTO @hierarchy
	                    (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, ValueType)
	                    SELECT @Name, @SequenceNo, @Parent_ID, @value, 'int'
	      if @Contents=' ' Select @SequenceNo=0
	    END
	  END
	INSERT INTO @hierarchy (Name, SequenceNo, Parent_ID, StringValue, Object_ID, ValueType)
	  SELECT '-',1, NULL, '', @Parent_ID-1, @type
	/**/
	   RETURN
	END

-- [dbo].[fnPersianToGregorianDate]




/*RZ960921*/
/*RZ14000126- Set null to string empty*/
Create FUNCTION [dbo].[fnPersianToGregorianDate] 
(
@DateWithOutSlash NVARCHAR(8), 
@Time NVARCHAR(8)
)
/*
	Select dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate( N'13960912', null)			
*/
Returns DATETIME
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	/*RZ14000126- Set null to string empty*/
	IF(ISNULL(@DateWithOutSlash,'') = '')
		SET @DateWithOutSlash = NULL
	/*RZ14000126- End*/


	DECLARE @iYear INT,@iMonth INT,@iDay INT
	SET @iYear = (SELECT CAST ((SUBSTRING(@DateWithOutSlash, 1, 4)) AS INT))
	SET @iMonth = (SELECT CAST ((SUBSTRING(@DateWithOutSlash, 5, 2)) AS INT))
	SET @iDay = (SELECT CAST ((SUBSTRING(@DateWithOutSlash, 7, 2)) AS INT))
	/*convert jalali*/
	DECLARE @jdn BIGINT
	SET @jdn = dbo.fnPersianToJulianDate(@iYear,@iMonth,@iDay)	
	/**/
    DECLARE @Jofst  AS NUMERIC(18,2)
    SET @Jofst=2415020.5
    RETURN CAST ((Convert(NVARCHAR(11),Convert(DATETIME,(@jdn- @Jofst),113),110)) + ' ' + ISNULL(@Time, '') AS DATETIME)          	            
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation]






/*RZ14020626*/
/*RZ14020927- Add PriceRoundType*/
Create FUNCTION [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation] (@Num NUMERIC(18,2))
/*
	SELECT dbo.[fnRoundNumberCalculation](null)
	SELECT dbo.[fnRoundNumberCalculation](5.0002)
	SELECT dbo.[fnRoundNumberCalculation](5.49)
	SELECT dbo.[fnRoundNumberCalculation](5.51)
	SELECT dbo.[fnRoundNumberCalculation](0.48)
	SELECT dbo.[fnRoundNumberCalculation](0.5)
*/
RETURNS NUMERIC(18,2)
AS
BEGIN
/**/	
	DECLARE @RoundType INT = 0
	/*RETURN CEILING(@Num)*/
	/*RETURN ROUND(@Num,@RoundType)*/
	/*-----------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14020927- Add PriceRoundType*/
	DECLARE @PriceRoundType INT = 0,@RoundNumberResult NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL 
	SET @PriceRoundType = (SELECT TOP(1) PriceRoundType FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
	
	IF(ISNULL(@PriceRoundType,0) = -1)
	BEGIN
		SET @RoundNumberResult =  FLOOR(@Num)
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PriceRoundType,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @RoundNumberResult =  ROUND(@Num,@RoundType)
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PriceRoundType,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @RoundNumberResult =  CEILING(@Num)
	END	
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @RoundNumberResult =  ROUND(@Num,@RoundType)
	END
	/**/
	RETURN @RoundNumberResult
	/*RZ14020927- End*/
	/*-----------------------------------*/
/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[fnSplitString]

/*9502*/
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[fnSplitString] 
( 
    @string NVARCHAR(MAX), 
    @delimiter CHAR(1) 
) 
RETURNS @output TABLE(splitdata NVARCHAR(MAX) 
) 
BEGIN 
    DECLARE @start INT, @end INT 
    SELECT @start = 1, @end = CHARINDEX(@delimiter, @string) 
    WHILE @start < LEN(@string) + 1 BEGIN 
        IF @end = 0  
            SET @end = LEN(@string) + 1
       
        INSERT INTO @output (splitdata)  
        VALUES(SUBSTRING(@string, @start, @end - @start)) 
        SET @start = @end + 1 
        SET @end = CHARINDEX(@delimiter, @string, @start)
        
    END 
    RETURN 
END

-- [dbo].[MobfnReturnOrderSumFooter]




/*RZ971011*/
/*RZ971114- Change price to decimals*/
/*AI14000803 - Remove ISNUlls*/
/*AI14001030- Add TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount*/
CREATE Function [dbo].[MobfnReturnOrderSumFooter]
(			
/*  
	Select * from dbo.MobfnReturnOrderSumFooter(16910) 	
*/
@OrderId INT 
)
Returns table
AS
Return
(
	SELECT 
		/*CAST(
		/-*Round(*-//-*RZ971114- Change price to decimals*-/
		SUM(
		  (ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)) *
		( ISNULL(ordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(ordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(ordIRound, 0)) 			
			+ ISNULL(ordIRoundPlus, 0) )		
			+ ISNULL(ordITax, 0) * ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)		
		) /-*, 0)*-/ AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumPriceAmount*/


		/*AI14001030- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
		CAST(SUM(ISNULL(ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(ordITaxSum,0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumPriceAmount
		/*AI14001030- End*/


		,CAST(SUM(ordIPrice) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumPrice
		,CAST(SUM(ordIQuantity) AS NUMERIC(18,10)) SumQuantity
		,CAST(SUM(ordIDiscount) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumDiscount
		,CAST(SUM(ordIDiscountI) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumDiscountI
		,CAST(SUM(ordITax) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumTax
		,CAST(SUM(ordIFCR) AS NUMERIC(18,10)) SumFCR
		,CAST(SUM(ordIFCRI) AS NUMERIC(18,10)) SumFCRI
		,CAST(SUM(ordIRound) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumRound	
		/*AI14001030- Add fields*/
		,CAST(SUM(ordIPriceSum) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) TotalPriceSum	
		,CAST(SUM(ordIDiscountSum) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) TotalDiscountSum
		,CAST(SUM(ordIDiscountISum) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) TotalDiscountISum
		,CAST(SUM(ordITaxSum) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) TotalTaxSum
		/*AI14001030- End*/ 
		/*AI14001030- Add TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount*/
		,CAST(SUM(
			ISNULL(ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountSum,0)		
		) AS NUMERIC(18,2))TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount
		/*AI14001030- End*/	
	FROM dbo.WSOrderItems
	WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
	AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
)

-- [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]





/*AI14000725*/
/*AI14000812 - Modify condition in check status*/
/*AI14010709 - Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*AI14020420 - Add ordTspSettingId*/
/*AI14020627 - Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*AI14020813 - Change status*/
/*AI14020823 - Add condition for update ordStatus*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	EXEC MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts 16954,4,1065,1065	
*/
(
@OrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderItemsTax]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*AI14010709- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT ordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] 
												  WHERE OrderID = @OrderId))
/*AI14010709- End*/

/*AI14020420- Add ordTspSettingId*/
DECLARE @ordTspSettingId INT 
SET @ordTspSettingId = (SELECT TOP(1) TspSettingId FROM [dbo].[TspSetting])
UPDATE  [dbo].[WSOrder]
SET  ordTspSettingId = @ordTspSettingId
WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
AND ISNULL(ordTspSettingId,0) = 0
/*AI14020420- End*/

/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)

/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*AI14020325 - End*/

SELECT  /*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*Round(*/
								CAST(SUM(
									(ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)) *
									(ISNULL(ordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(ordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(ordIRound, 0)) 
									+ ISNULL(ordIRoundPlus, 0) )		
									+ ISNULL(ordITax, 0) * ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)		
								) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
								/*, 0)*/ ,0)*/
		/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
		@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM( ISNULL([ordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([ordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
		/*RZ991008- End*/
		/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,@SumQuantity = SUM(ordIQuantity)
		,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(ordIDiscountSum)
		,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(ordITaxSum)
		/*AI14020325 - End*/
FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @ordItemCount INT, @ordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTax NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordCalculateTax BIT,@ordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@ordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@ordRound NUMERIC(18,2),@ordStatusId INT,
	@ordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)
	/**/
	SELECT 
       @ordInstallAmount =[ordInstallAmount],
       @ordDiscount = [ordDiscount],
       @ordTaxIndex = [ordTaxIndex],
       @ordCalculateTax = [ordCalculateTax],
       @ordDiscountPercent = [ordDiscountPercent],
       @ordTransportation = [ordTransportation],
       @ordRound = [ordRound],
	   @ordStatusId= [ordStatusId] 
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O
	WHERE O.[OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	SET @ordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@ordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @ordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordTax,0) + ISNULL(@ordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@ordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @ordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*AI14010709- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN					
		/*AI14020627-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @ordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordItemsAmount))					
		SET @ordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumAmount))		
		SET @ordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTax))		
		SET @ordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTotalAmount))		
		SET @ordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordDiscount))
		/*AI14020325- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/	
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))	
		/*AI14020325- End*/
		/*AI14020627-End*/
	END
	/*AI14010709- End*/
	/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' ����' *//*RZ990902- Remove rial in ordTotalAmountStr*/
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
	SET  [ordItemCount] = ISNULL(@ordItemCount,0)
		,[ordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@ordItemsAmount,0)
		,[ordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0)
		,[ordTax] = ISNULL(@ordTax,0)
		,[ordTotalAmount] = @ordTotalAmount
		,[ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStr
		,[ordDiscount] = ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[ordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[ordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[ordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*AI14020325 - End*/
	WHERE OrderID = @OrderId
	/**/
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @ordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*//*RZ990902- Remove rial in ordTotalAmountStr*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @OrdIdLang INT
				SET @OrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@OrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						SET [ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSOrderLangID] = @OrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						([OrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@OrderId
						,@LangId
						,@ordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/
	/*================= Update status ==================*/	
	DECLARE @SumOrdPayAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
	SET @SumOrdPayAmount = ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL( SUM(ordpayAmount), 0) sumordpayAmount
									FROM dbo.WSOrderPayments
									WHERE ordpayOrderId = @OrderId
									AND ISNULL(ordpayDeActive,0) = 0 /*AI14020823*/
									),0)
	/*IF(@SumOrdPayAmount IS NOT NULL AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))*/
	IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdPayAmount,0) <> 0 AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))/*AI14000812 - Modify condition in check status*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@SumOrdPayAmount = 0 AND @ordStatusId <>1)
			SET @ordStatusId = 1
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount < @ordTotalAmount) /*AI14020813 - Change status - <>*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 2
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount >= @ordTotalAmount) /*AI14020813 - Change status - =*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 3
		/**/	 		
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
		SET [ordStatusId] = @ordStatusId 
		WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	END
	/*==================================================*/
END	
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030126- Add calculate OrderOtherAmounts*/
DECLARE @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts  INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderOtherAmounts]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@CurrentDate = NULL,
@Result = @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts OUTPUT	
/*RZ14030126- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]






/*RZ980231*/
/*AI981221 Created*/
/*AI991014 - change SumPriceAmount calc way*/
/*AI14010707 - Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*AI14020627 - Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	EXEC MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts 2149,2,1033,1065	
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*AI14010707- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId))
/*AI14010707- End*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/

/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*AI14020325 - End*/

DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
SELECT 
	/*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*ROUND(*/
							CAST(SUM(
							 (ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1)) 
							 * 
							 (( ISNULL(pordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(pordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(pordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(pordIRound, 0))
							 + ISNULL(pordIRoundPlus, 0)
							 ))	  
	 							+ (ISNULL( pordITax, 0) * ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1))
							) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
							/*, 0)*/,0)		*/ /*AI991014*/
	@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0) /*AI991014*/
	/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	,@SumQuantity = SUM(pordIQuantity)
	,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(pordIDiscountSum)
	,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(pordITaxSum)
	/*AI14020325 - End*/	
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum, 0) = 0
/**/
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @pordItemCount  INT, @pordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTax NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordCalculateTax BIT,@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@pordRound NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)	
	/**/			
	SELECT 
       @pordInstallAmount =[pordInstallAmount],
       @pordDiscount = [pordDiscount],
       @pordTaxIndex = [pordTaxIndex],
       @pordCalculateTax = [pordCalculateTax],
       @pordDiscountPercent = [pordDiscountPercent],
       @pordTransportation = [pordTransportation],
       @pordRound = [pordRound]      
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
	WHERE PO.[PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	SET @pordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@pordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @pordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@pordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordTax,0) + ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@pordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @pordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*AI14010707- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN					
		/*AI14020627-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @pordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsAmount))					
		SET @pordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordSumAmount))		
		SET @pordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTax))		
		SET @pordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTotalAmount))		
		SET @pordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordDiscount))	
		
		/*AI14020325- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/	
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))	
		/*AI14020325- End*/
		/*AI14020627- End*/
	END
	/*AI14010707- End*/




	/*************** ToTalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' ����'*/
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL
